Signature: org.jfree.chart.renderer.category.StatisticalBarRenderer.drawHorizontalItem(Graphics2D,CategoryItemRendererState,Rectangle2D,CategoryPlot,CategoryAxis,ValueAxis,StatisticalCategoryDataset,int,int)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/renderer/category/StatisticalBarRenderer.java
Original range: 230-358
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws an item for a plot with a horizontal orientation.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param state  the renderer state.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param plot  the plot.
     * @param domainAxis  the domain axis.
     * @param rangeAxis  the range axis.
     * @param dataset  the data.
     * @param row  the row index (zero-based).
     * @param column  the column index (zero-based).
     */
Method code:
230 |     protected void drawHorizontalItem(Graphics2D g2,
231 |                                       CategoryItemRendererState state,
232 |                                       Rectangle2D dataArea,
233 |                                       CategoryPlot plot,
234 |                                       CategoryAxis domainAxis,
235 |                                       ValueAxis rangeAxis,
236 |                                       StatisticalCategoryDataset dataset,
237 |                                       int row,
238 |                                       int column) {
239 |                                      
240 |         RectangleEdge xAxisLocation = plot.getDomainAxisEdge();
241 |         
242 |         // BAR Y
243 |         double rectY = domainAxis.getCategoryStart(column, getColumnCount(), 
244 |                 dataArea, xAxisLocation);
245 | 
246 |         int seriesCount = getRowCount();
247 |         int categoryCount = getColumnCount();
248 |         if (seriesCount > 1) {
249 |             double seriesGap = dataArea.getHeight() * getItemMargin()
250 |                                / (categoryCount * (seriesCount - 1));
251 |             rectY = rectY + row * (state.getBarWidth() + seriesGap);
252 |         }
253 |         else {
254 |             rectY = rectY + row * state.getBarWidth();
255 |         }
256 | 
257 |         // BAR X
258 |         Number meanValue = dataset.getMeanValue(row, column);
259 | 
260 |         double value = meanValue.doubleValue();
261 |         double base = 0.0;
262 |         double lclip = getLowerClip();
263 |         double uclip = getUpperClip();
264 | 
265 |         if (uclip <= 0.0) {  // cases 1, 2, 3 and 4
266 |             if (value >= uclip) {
267 |                 return; // bar is not visible
268 |             }
269 |             base = uclip;
270 |             if (value <= lclip) {
271 |                 value = lclip;
272 |             }
273 |         }
274 |         else if (lclip <= 0.0) { // cases 5, 6, 7 and 8
275 |             if (value >= uclip) {
276 |                 value = uclip;
277 |             }
278 |             else {
279 |                 if (value <= lclip) {
280 |                     value = lclip;
281 |                 }
282 |             }
283 |         }
284 |         else { // cases 9, 10, 11 and 12
285 |             if (value <= lclip) {
286 |                 return; // bar is not visible
287 |             }
288 |             base = getLowerClip();
289 |             if (value >= uclip) {
290 |                value = uclip;
291 |             }
292 |         }
293 | 
294 |         RectangleEdge yAxisLocation = plot.getRangeAxisEdge();
295 |         double transY1 = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(base, dataArea, yAxisLocation);
296 |         double transY2 = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(value, dataArea, 
297 |                 yAxisLocation);
298 |         double rectX = Math.min(transY2, transY1);
299 | 
300 |         double rectHeight = state.getBarWidth();
301 |         double rectWidth = Math.abs(transY2 - transY1);
302 | 
303 |         Rectangle2D bar = new Rectangle2D.Double(rectX, rectY, rectWidth, 
304 |                 rectHeight);
305 |         Paint seriesPaint = getItemPaint(row, column);
306 |         g2.setPaint(seriesPaint);
307 |         g2.fill(bar);
308 |         if (isDrawBarOutline() && state.getBarWidth() > 3) {
309 |             g2.setStroke(getItemStroke(row, column));
310 |             g2.setPaint(getItemOutlinePaint(row, column));
311 |             g2.draw(bar);
312 |         }
313 | 
314 |         // standard deviation lines
315 |             double valueDelta = dataset.getStdDevValue(row, column).doubleValue();
316 |             double highVal = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(meanValue.doubleValue() 
317 |                     + valueDelta, dataArea, yAxisLocation);
318 |             double lowVal = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(meanValue.doubleValue() 
319 |                     - valueDelta, dataArea, yAxisLocation);
320 | 
321 |             if (this.errorIndicatorStroke != null) {
322 |                 g2.setStroke(this.errorIndicatorStroke);
323 |             }
324 |             else {
325 |                 g2.setStroke(getItemOutlineStroke(row, column));
326 |             }
327 |             if (this.errorIndicatorPaint != null) {
328 |                 g2.setPaint(this.errorIndicatorPaint);  
329 |             }
330 |             else {
331 |                 g2.setPaint(getItemOutlinePaint(row, column));   
332 |             }
333 |         
334 |             Line2D line = null;
335 |             line = new Line2D.Double(lowVal, rectY + rectHeight / 2.0d, 
336 |                                      highVal, rectY + rectHeight / 2.0d);
337 |             g2.draw(line);
338 |             line = new Line2D.Double(highVal, rectY + rectHeight * 0.25, 
339 |                                      highVal, rectY + rectHeight * 0.75);
340 |             g2.draw(line);
341 |             line = new Line2D.Double(lowVal, rectY + rectHeight * 0.25, 
342 |                                      lowVal, rectY + rectHeight * 0.75);
343 |             g2.draw(line);
344 |         
345 |         CategoryItemLabelGenerator generator = getItemLabelGenerator(row, 
346 |                 column);
347 |         if (generator != null && isItemLabelVisible(row, column)) {
348 |             drawItemLabel(g2, dataset, row, column, plot, generator, bar, 
349 |                     (value < 0.0));
350 |         }        
351 | 
352 |         // add an item entity, if this information is being collected
353 |         EntityCollection entities = state.getEntityCollection();
354 |         if (entities != null) {
355 |             addItemEntity(entities, dataset, row, column, bar);
356 |         }
357 | 
358 |     }

     * Draws an axis line at the current cursor position and edge.

521 |     protected void drawAxisLine(Graphics2D g2, double cursor,
522 |                                 Rectangle2D dataArea, RectangleEdge edge) {
523 |         Line2D axisLine = null;
524 |         if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
525 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(dataArea.getX(), cursor, 
526 |                     dataArea.getMaxX(), cursor);  
527 |         }
528 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
529 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(dataArea.getX(), cursor, 
530 |                     dataArea.getMaxX(), cursor);  
531 |         }
532 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
533 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(cursor, dataArea.getY(), cursor, 
534 |                     dataArea.getMaxY());  
535 |         }
536 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
537 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(cursor, dataArea.getY(), cursor, 
538 |                     dataArea.getMaxY());  
539 |         }
540 |         g2.setPaint(getAxisLinePaint());
541 |         g2.setStroke(getAxisLineStroke());
542 |         g2.draw(axisLine);
543 |         
544 |         boolean drawUpOrRight = false;  
545 |         boolean drawDownOrLeft = false;
546 |         if (this.positiveArrowVisible) {
547 |             if (this.inverted) {
548 |                 drawDownOrLeft = true;   
549 |             }
550 |             else {
551 |                 drawUpOrRight = true;   
552 |             }
553 |         }
554 |         if (this.negativeArrowVisible) {
555 |             if (this.inverted) {
556 |                 drawUpOrRight = true;   
557 |             }
558 |             else {
559 |                 drawDownOrLeft = true;   
560 |             }
561 |         }
562 |         if (drawUpOrRight) {

564 |             double y = 0.0;
565 |             Shape arrow = null;
566 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP || edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
567 |                 x = dataArea.getMaxX();
568 |                 y = cursor;
569 |                 arrow = this.rightArrow; 
570 |             }
571 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT 
572 |                     || edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
573 |                 x = cursor;
574 |                 y = dataArea.getMinY();
575 |                 arrow = this.upArrow; 
576 |             }
577 | 
578 |             // draw the arrow...
579 |             AffineTransform transformer = new AffineTransform();
580 |             transformer.setToTranslation(x, y);
581 |             Shape shape = transformer.createTransformedShape(arrow);
582 |             g2.fill(shape);
583 |             g2.draw(shape);
584 |         }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.ObjectUtilities.isJDK14()
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/ObjectUtilities.java
Original range: 440-471
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns <code>true</code> if we are running on JRE 1.4 or later.
     * 
     * @return A boolean.
     */
Method code:
440 |     public static boolean isJDK14() {
441 |         ClassLoader loader = getClassLoader(ObjectUtilities.class);
442 |         if (loader != null) {
443 |             try {
444 |               loader.loadClass("java.util.RandomAccess");
445 |               return true;
446 |             }
447 |             catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
448 |               return false;
449 |             }
450 |             catch(Exception e) {
451 |               // do nothing, but do not crash ...
452 |             }
453 |         }
454 |         // OK, the quick and dirty, but secure way failed. Lets try it
455 |         // using the standard way.
456 |         try {
457 |             String version = System.getProperty(
458 |                     "java.vm.specification.version");
459 |             // parse the beast...
460 |             if (version == null) {
461 |                 return false;
462 |             }
463 | 
464 |             String[] versions = parseVersions(version);
465 |             String[] target = new String[]{ "1", "4" };
466 |             return (ArrayUtilities.compareVersionArrays(versions, target) >= 0);
467 |         }
468 |         catch(Exception e) {
469 |             return false;
470 |         }
471 |     }

===== M0023 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.CategoryPlot.draw(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,Point2D,PlotState,PlotRenderingInfo)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/CategoryPlot.java
Original range: 2552-2722
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws the plot on a Java 2D graphics device (such as the screen or a 
     * printer).
     * <P>
     * At your option, you may supply an instance of {@link PlotRenderingInfo}.
     * If you do, it will be populated with information about the drawing,
     * including various plot dimensions and tooltip info.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param area  the area within which the plot (including axes) should 
     *              be drawn.
     * @param anchor  the anchor point (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param parentState  the state from the parent plot, if there is one.
     * @param state  collects info as the chart is drawn (possibly 
     *               <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
2552 |     public void draw(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area, 
2553 |                      Point2D anchor,
2554 |                      PlotState parentState,
2555 |                      PlotRenderingInfo state) {
2556 | 
2557 |         // if the plot area is too small, just return...
2558 |         boolean b1 = (area.getWidth() <= MINIMUM_WIDTH_TO_DRAW);
2559 |         boolean b2 = (area.getHeight() <= MINIMUM_HEIGHT_TO_DRAW);
2560 |         if (b1 || b2) {
2561 |             return;
2562 |         }
2563 | 
2564 |         // record the plot area...
2565 |         if (state == null) {
2566 |             // if the incoming state is null, no information will be passed
2567 |             // back to the caller - but we create a temporary state to record
2568 |             // the plot area, since that is used later by the axes
2569 |             state = new PlotRenderingInfo(null);
2570 |         }
2571 |         state.setPlotArea(area);
2572 | 
2573 |         // adjust the drawing area for the plot insets (if any)...
2574 |         RectangleInsets insets = getInsets();
2575 |         insets.trim(area);
2576 | 
2577 |         // calculate the data area...
2578 |         AxisSpace space = calculateAxisSpace(g2, area);
2579 |         Rectangle2D dataArea = space.shrink(area, null);
2580 |         this.axisOffset.trim(dataArea);
2581 | 
2582 |         state.setDataArea(dataArea);
2583 | 
2584 |         // if there is a renderer, it draws the background, otherwise use the 
2585 |         // default background...
2586 |         if (getRenderer() != null) {
2587 |             getRenderer().drawBackground(g2, this, dataArea);
2588 |         }
2589 |         else {
2590 |             drawBackground(g2, dataArea);
2591 |         }
2592 |        
2593 |         Map axisStateMap = drawAxes(g2, area, dataArea, state);
2594 | 
2595 |         // don't let anyone draw outside the data area
2596 |         Shape savedClip = g2.getClip();
2597 |         g2.clip(dataArea);
2598 | 
2599 |         drawDomainGridlines(g2, dataArea);
2600 | 
2601 |         AxisState rangeAxisState = (AxisState) axisStateMap.get(getRangeAxis());
2602 |         if (rangeAxisState == null) {
2603 |             if (parentState != null) {
2604 |                 rangeAxisState = (AxisState) parentState.getSharedAxisStates()
2605 |                         .get(getRangeAxis());
2606 |             }
2607 |         }
2608 |         if (rangeAxisState != null) {
2609 |             drawRangeGridlines(g2, dataArea, rangeAxisState.getTicks());
2610 |         }
2611 |         
2612 |         // draw the markers...
2613 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2614 |             drawDomainMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.BACKGROUND);
2615 |         }        
2616 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2617 |             drawRangeMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.BACKGROUND);
2618 |         }
2619 | 
2620 |         // now render data items...
2621 |         boolean foundData = false;
2622 | 
2623 |         // set up the alpha-transparency...
2624 |         Composite originalComposite = g2.getComposite();
2625 |         g2.setComposite(AlphaComposite.getInstance(
2626 |                 AlphaComposite.SRC_OVER, getForegroundAlpha()));
2627 | 
2628 |         DatasetRenderingOrder order = getDatasetRenderingOrder();
2629 |         if (order == DatasetRenderingOrder.FORWARD) {
2630 | 
2631 |             // draw background annotations
2632 |             int datasetCount = this.datasets.size();
2633 |             for (int i = 0; i < datasetCount; i++) {
2634 |                 CategoryItemRenderer r = getRenderer(i);
2635 |                 if (r != null) {
2636 |                     CategoryAxis domainAxis = getDomainAxisForDataset(i);
2637 |                     ValueAxis rangeAxis = getRangeAxisForDataset(i);
2638 |                     r.drawAnnotations(g2, dataArea, domainAxis, rangeAxis,
2639 |                             Layer.BACKGROUND, state);
2640 |                 }
2641 |             }
2642 | 
2643 |             for (int i = 0; i < datasetCount; i++) {
2644 |                 foundData = render(g2, dataArea, i, state) || foundData;
2645 |             }
2646 | 
2647 |             // draw foreground annotations
2648 |             for (int i = 0; i < datasetCount; i++) {
2649 |                 CategoryItemRenderer r = getRenderer(i);
2650 |                 if (r != null) {
2651 |                     CategoryAxis domainAxis = getDomainAxisForDataset(i);
2652 |                     ValueAxis rangeAxis = getRangeAxisForDataset(i);
2653 |                     r.drawAnnotations(g2, dataArea, domainAxis, rangeAxis,
2654 |                             Layer.FOREGROUND, state);
2655 |                 }
2656 |             }
2657 |         }
2658 |         else {  // DatasetRenderingOrder.REVERSE
2659 |             
2660 |             // draw background annotations
2661 |             int datasetCount = this.datasets.size();
2662 |             for (int i = datasetCount - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
2663 |                 CategoryItemRenderer r = getRenderer(i);
2664 |                 if (r != null) {
2665 |                     CategoryAxis domainAxis = getDomainAxisForDataset(i);
2666 |                     ValueAxis rangeAxis = getRangeAxisForDataset(i);
2667 |                     r.drawAnnotations(g2, dataArea, domainAxis, rangeAxis,
2668 |                             Layer.BACKGROUND, state);
2669 |                 }
2670 |             }
2671 |             
2672 |             for (int i = this.datasets.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
2673 |                 foundData = render(g2, dataArea, i, state) || foundData;   
2674 |             }
2675 | 
2676 |             // draw foreground annotations
2677 |             for (int i = datasetCount - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
2678 |                 CategoryItemRenderer r = getRenderer(i);
2679 |                 if (r != null) {
2680 |                     CategoryAxis domainAxis = getDomainAxisForDataset(i);
2681 |                     ValueAxis rangeAxis = getRangeAxisForDataset(i);
2682 |                     r.drawAnnotations(g2, dataArea, domainAxis, rangeAxis,
2683 |                             Layer.FOREGROUND, state);
2684 |                 }
2685 |             }
2686 |         }
2687 |         
2688 |         // draw the foreground markers...
2689 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2690 |             drawDomainMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.FOREGROUND);
2691 |         }
2692 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2693 |             drawRangeMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.FOREGROUND);
2694 |         }
2695 | 
2696 |         // draw the plot's annotations (if any)...
2697 |         drawAnnotations(g2, dataArea, state);
2698 | 
2699 |         g2.setClip(savedClip);
2700 |         g2.setComposite(originalComposite);
2701 | 
2702 |         if (!foundData) {
2703 |             drawNoDataMessage(g2, dataArea);
2704 |         }
2705 | 
2706 |         // draw range crosshair if required...
2707 |         if (isRangeCrosshairVisible()) {
2708 |             // FIXME: this doesn't handle multiple range axes
2709 |             drawRangeCrosshair(g2, dataArea, getOrientation(), 
2710 |                     getRangeCrosshairValue(), getRangeAxis(),
2711 |                     getRangeCrosshairStroke(), getRangeCrosshairPaint());
2712 |         }
2713 | 
2714 |         // draw an outline around the plot area...
2715 |         if (getRenderer() != null) {
2716 |             getRenderer().drawOutline(g2, this, dataArea);
2717 |         }
2718 |         else {
2719 |             drawOutline(g2, dataArea);
2720 |         }
2721 | 
2722 |     }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.Plot.resolveDomainAxisLocation(AxisLocation,PlotOrientation)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/Plot.java
Original range: 1305-1355
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolves a domain axis location for a given plot orientation.
     *
     * @param location  the location (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param orientation  the orientation (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The edge (never <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
1305 |     public static RectangleEdge resolveDomainAxisLocation(
1306 |             AxisLocation location, PlotOrientation orientation) {
1307 |         
1308 |         if (location == null) {
1309 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'location' argument.");   
1310 |         }
1311 |         if (orientation == null) {
1312 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'orientation' argument.");
1313 |         }
1314 | 
1315 |         RectangleEdge result = null;
1316 |         
1317 |         if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_RIGHT) {
1318 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1319 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1320 |             }
1321 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1322 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1323 |             }
1324 |         }
1325 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_LEFT) {
1326 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1327 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1328 |             }
1329 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1330 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1331 |             }
1332 |         }
1333 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_RIGHT) {
1334 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1335 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1336 |             }
1337 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1338 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1339 |             }
1340 |         }
1341 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_LEFT) {
1342 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1343 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1344 |             }
1345 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1346 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1347 |             }
1348 |         }
1349 |         // the above should cover all the options...
1350 |         if (result == null) {
1351 |             throw new IllegalStateException("resolveDomainAxisLocation()");
1352 |         }
1353 |         return result;
1354 |         
1355 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextBlock.calculateOffsets(TextBlockAnchor,double,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextBlock.java
Original range: 266-311
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the x and y offsets required to align the text block with the
     * specified anchor point.  This assumes that the top left of the text 
     * block is at (0.0, 0.0).
     * 
     * @param anchor  the anchor position.
     * @param width  the width of the text block.
     * @param height  the height of the text block.
     * 
     * @return The offsets (float[0] = x offset, float[1] = y offset).
     */
Method code:
266 |     private float[] calculateOffsets(TextBlockAnchor anchor, 
267 |                                      double width, double height) {
268 |         float[] result = new float[2];
269 |         float xAdj = 0.0f;
270 |         float yAdj = 0.0f;
271 | 
272 |         if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_CENTER
273 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER
274 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER) {
275 |                     
276 |             xAdj = (float) -width / 2.0f;
277 |             
278 |         }
279 |         else if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_RIGHT
280 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT
281 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
282 |                     
283 |             xAdj = (float) -width;
284 |             
285 |         }
286 | 
287 |         if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_LEFT
288 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_CENTER
289 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_RIGHT) {
290 |                     
291 |             yAdj = 0.0f;
292 |             
293 |         }
294 |         else if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
295 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER
296 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT) {
297 |                     
298 |             yAdj = (float) -height / 2.0f;
299 |             
300 |         }
301 |         else if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
302 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
303 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
304 |                     
305 |             yAdj = (float) -height;
306 |             
307 |         }
308 |         result[0] = xAdj;
309 |         result[1] = yAdj;
310 |         return result;
311 |     }

/**
     * Arranges the contents of the block, within the given constraints, and 
     * returns the block size.

===== M0054 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.BlockBorder.draw(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/BlockBorder.java
Original range: 165-194
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws the border by filling in the reserved space.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param area  the area.
     */
Method code:
165 |     public void draw(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area) {
166 |         // this default implementation will just fill the available
167 |         // border space with a single color
168 |         double t = this.insets.calculateTopInset(area.getHeight());
169 |         double b = this.insets.calculateBottomInset(area.getHeight());
170 |         double l = this.insets.calculateLeftInset(area.getWidth());
171 |         double r = this.insets.calculateRightInset(area.getWidth());
172 |         double x = area.getX();
173 |         double y = area.getY();
174 |         double w = area.getWidth();
175 |         double h = area.getHeight();
176 |         g2.setPaint(this.paint);
177 |         Rectangle2D rect = new Rectangle2D.Double();
178 |         if (t > 0.0) {
179 |             rect.setRect(x, y, w, t);
180 |             g2.fill(rect);
181 |         }
182 |         if (b > 0.0) {
183 |             rect.setRect(x, y + h - b, w, b);
184 |             g2.fill(rect);
185 |         }
186 |         if (l > 0.0) {
187 |             rect.setRect(x, y, l, h);
188 |             g2.fill(rect);
189 |         }
190 |         if (r > 0.0) {
191 |             rect.setRect(x + w - r, y, r, h);
192 |             g2.fill(rect);
193 |         }
194 |     }

===== M0059 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.statistics.DefaultStatisticalCategoryDataset.add(Number,Number,Comparable,Comparable)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/statistics/DefaultStatisticalCategoryDataset.java
Original range: 297-340
Documentation:
/**
     * Adds a mean and standard deviation to the table.
     *
     * @param mean  the mean.
     * @param standardDeviation  the standard deviation.
     * @param rowKey  the row key.
     * @param columnKey  the column key.
     */
Method code:
297 |     public void add(Number mean, Number standardDeviation,
298 |                     Comparable rowKey, Comparable columnKey) {
299 |         MeanAndStandardDeviation item = new MeanAndStandardDeviation(
300 |                 mean, standardDeviation);
301 |         this.data.addObject(item, rowKey, columnKey);
302 |         double m = 0.0;
303 |         double sd = 0.0;
304 |         if (mean != null) {
305 |             m = mean.doubleValue();
306 |         }
307 |         if (standardDeviation != null) {
308 |             sd = standardDeviation.doubleValue();   
309 |         }
310 |         
311 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m)) {
312 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.maximumRangeValue) 
313 |                     || m > this.maximumRangeValue) {
314 |                 this.maximumRangeValue = m;
315 |             }
316 |         }
317 |         
318 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m + sd)) {
319 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.maximumRangeValueIncStdDev) 
320 |                     || (m + sd) > this.maximumRangeValueIncStdDev) {
321 |                 this.maximumRangeValueIncStdDev = m + sd;
322 |             }
323 |         }
324 | 
325 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m)) {
326 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.minimumRangeValue) 
327 |                     || m < this.minimumRangeValue) {
328 |                 this.minimumRangeValue = m;
329 |             }
330 |         }
331 | 
332 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m - sd)) {
333 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.minimumRangeValueIncStdDev) 
334 |                     || (m - sd) < this.minimumRangeValueIncStdDev) {
335 |                 this.minimumRangeValueIncStdDev = m - sd;
336 |             }
337 |         }
338 | 
339 |         fireDatasetChanged();
340 |     }

/**
     * Compares this tick unit to an arbitrary object.
     *
     * @param object  the object to compare against.
     *
     * @return <code>1</code> if the size of the other object is less than this,
     *      <code>0</code> if both have the same size and <code>-1</code> this

===== M0065 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.renderer.category.BarRenderer.calculateBarWidth(CategoryPlot,Rectangle2D,int,CategoryItemRendererState)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/renderer/category/BarRenderer.java
Original range: 515-552
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the bar width and stores it in the renderer state.
     * 
     * @param plot  the plot.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param rendererIndex  the renderer index.
     * @param state  the renderer state.
     */
Method code:
515 |     protected void calculateBarWidth(CategoryPlot plot, 
516 |                                      Rectangle2D dataArea, 
517 |                                      int rendererIndex,
518 |                                      CategoryItemRendererState state) {
519 |                                          
520 |         CategoryAxis domainAxis = getDomainAxis(plot, rendererIndex);
521 |         CategoryDataset dataset = plot.getDataset(rendererIndex);
522 |         if (dataset != null) {
523 |             int columns = dataset.getColumnCount();
524 |             int rows = dataset.getRowCount();
525 |             double space = 0.0;
526 |             PlotOrientation orientation = plot.getOrientation();
527 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
528 |                 space = dataArea.getHeight();
529 |             }
530 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
531 |                 space = dataArea.getWidth();
532 |             }
533 |             double maxWidth = space * getMaximumBarWidth();
534 |             double categoryMargin = 0.0;
535 |             double currentItemMargin = 0.0;
536 |             if (columns > 1) {
537 |                 categoryMargin = domainAxis.getCategoryMargin();
538 |             }
539 |             if (rows > 1) {
540 |                 currentItemMargin = getItemMargin();
541 |             }
542 |             double used = space * (1 - domainAxis.getLowerMargin() 
543 |                                      - domainAxis.getUpperMargin()
544 |                                      - categoryMargin - currentItemMargin);
545 |             if ((rows * columns) > 0) {
546 |                 state.setBarWidth(Math.min(used / (rows * columns), maxWidth));
547 |             }
548 |             else {
549 |                 state.setBarWidth(Math.min(used, maxWidth));
550 |             }
551 |         }
552 |     }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.valueToJava2D(double,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 523-549
Documentation:
/**
     * Converts a data value to a coordinate in Java2D space, assuming that the
     * axis runs along one edge of the specified dataArea.
     * <p>
     * Note that it is possible for the coordinate to fall outside the plotArea.
     *
     * @param value  the data value.
     * @param area  the area for plotting the data.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The Java2D coordinate.
     * 
     * @see #java2DToValue(double, Rectangle2D, RectangleEdge)
     */
Method code:
523 |     public double valueToJava2D(double value, Rectangle2D area, 
524 |                                 RectangleEdge edge) {
525 |         
526 |         Range range = getRange();
527 |         double axisMin = range.getLowerBound();
528 |         double axisMax = range.getUpperBound();
529 | 
530 |         double min = 0.0;
531 |         double max = 0.0;
532 |         if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
533 |             min = area.getX();
534 |             max = area.getMaxX();
535 |         }
536 |         else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
537 |             max = area.getMinY();
538 |             min = area.getMaxY();
539 |         }
540 |         if (isInverted()) {
541 |             return max 
542 |                    - ((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) * (max - min);
543 |         }
544 |         else {
545 |             return min 
546 |                    + ((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) * (max - min);
547 |         }
548 | 
549 |     }

===== M0083 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.nextLineBreak(String,int,float,BreakIterator,TextMeasurer)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 236-274
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the character index of the next line break.
     * 
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param start  the start index.
     * @param width  the target display width.
     * @param iterator  the word break iterator.
     * @param measurer  the text measurer.
     * 
     * @return The index of the next line break.
     */
Method code:
236 |     private static int nextLineBreak(String text, int start,
237 |             float width, BreakIterator iterator, TextMeasurer measurer) {
238 |         
239 |         // this method is (loosely) based on code in JFreeReport's 
240 |         // TextParagraph class
241 |         int current = start;
242 |         int end;
243 |         float x = 0.0f;
244 |         boolean firstWord = true;
245 |         int newline = text.indexOf('\n', start);
246 |         if (newline < 0) {
247 |             newline = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
248 |         }
249 |         while (((end = iterator.next()) != BreakIterator.DONE)) {
250 |             if (end > newline) {
251 |                 return newline;
252 |             }
253 |             x += measurer.getStringWidth(text, current, end);
254 |             if (x > width) {
255 |                 if (firstWord) {
256 |                     while (measurer.getStringWidth(text, start, end) > width) {
257 |                         end--;
258 |                         if (end <= start) {
259 |                             return end;
260 |                         }
261 |                     }
262 |                     return end;
263 |                 }
264 |                 else {
265 |                     end = iterator.previous();
266 |                     return end;
267 |                 }
268 |             }
269 |             // we found at least one word that fits ...
270 |             firstWord = false;
271 |             current = end;
272 |         }
273 |         return BreakIterator.DONE;
274 |     }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.ValueAxis.reserveSpace(Graphics2D,Plot,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge,AxisSpace)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/ValueAxis.java
Original range: 751-802
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the space required to draw the axis.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param plot  the plot that the axis belongs to.
     * @param plotArea  the area within which the plot should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     * @param space  the space already reserved (for other axes).
     *
     * @return The space required to draw the axis (including pre-reserved 
     *         space).
     */
Method code:
751 |     public AxisSpace reserveSpace(Graphics2D g2, Plot plot,
752 |                                   Rectangle2D plotArea, 
753 |                                   RectangleEdge edge, AxisSpace space) {
754 | 
755 |         // create a new space object if one wasn't supplied...
756 |         if (space == null) {
757 |             space = new AxisSpace();
758 |         }
759 |         
760 |         // if the axis is not visible, no additional space is required...
761 |         if (!isVisible()) {
762 |             return space;
763 |         }
764 | 
765 |         // if the axis has a fixed dimension, return it...
766 |         double dimension = getFixedDimension();
767 |         if (dimension > 0.0) {
768 |             space.ensureAtLeast(dimension, edge);
769 |         }
770 | 
771 |         // calculate the max size of the tick labels (if visible)...
772 |         double tickLabelHeight = 0.0;
773 |         double tickLabelWidth = 0.0;
774 |         if (isTickLabelsVisible()) {
775 |             g2.setFont(getTickLabelFont());
776 |             List ticks = refreshTicks(g2, new AxisState(), plotArea, edge);
777 |             if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
778 |                 tickLabelHeight = findMaximumTickLabelHeight(ticks, g2, 
779 |                         plotArea, isVerticalTickLabels());
780 |             }
781 |             else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
782 |                 tickLabelWidth = findMaximumTickLabelWidth(ticks, g2, plotArea,
783 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
784 |             }
785 |         }
786 | 
787 |         // get the axis label size and update the space object...
788 |         Rectangle2D labelEnclosure = getLabelEnclosure(g2, edge);
789 |         double labelHeight = 0.0;
790 |         double labelWidth = 0.0;
791 |         if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
792 |             labelHeight = labelEnclosure.getHeight();
793 |             space.add(labelHeight + tickLabelHeight, edge);
794 |         }
795 |         else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
796 |             labelWidth = labelEnclosure.getWidth();
797 |             space.add(labelWidth + tickLabelWidth, edge);
798 |         }
799 | 
800 |         return space;
801 | 
802 |     }

/**
     * Adds a marker for display by a particular renderer.
     * <P>
     * Typically a marker will be drawn by the renderer as a line perpendicular
     * to a range axis, however this is entirely up to the renderer.
     *
     * @param index  the renderer index.
     * @param marker  the marker.

2081 |     public void addRangeMarker(int index, Marker marker, Layer layer) {

Signature: org.jfree.chart.JFreeChart.drawTitle(Title,Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/JFreeChart.java
Original range: 1279-1347
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws a title.  The title should be drawn at the top, bottom, left or 
     * right of the specified area, and the area should be updated to reflect 
     * the amount of space used by the title.
     *
     * @param t  the title (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param g2  the graphics device (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param area  the chart area, excluding any existing titles 
     *              (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param entities  a flag that controls whether or not an entity 
     *                  collection is returned for the title.
     * 
     * @return An entity collection for the title (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
1279 |     protected EntityCollection drawTitle(Title t, Graphics2D g2, 
1280 |                                          Rectangle2D area, boolean entities) {
1281 | 
1282 |         if (t == null) {
1283 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 't' argument.");   
1284 |         }
1285 |         if (area == null) {
1286 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'area' argument.");   
1287 |         }
1288 |         Rectangle2D titleArea = new Rectangle2D.Double();
1289 |         RectangleEdge position = t.getPosition();
1290 |         double ww = area.getWidth();
1291 |         if (ww <= 0.0) {
1292 |             return null;
1293 |         }
1294 |         double hh = area.getHeight();
1295 |         if (hh <= 0.0) {
1296 |             return null;
1297 |         }
1298 |         RectangleConstraint constraint = new RectangleConstraint(ww, 
1299 |                 new Range(0.0, ww), LengthConstraintType.RANGE, hh, 
1300 |                 new Range(0.0, hh), LengthConstraintType.RANGE);
1301 |         Object retValue = null;
1302 |         BlockParams p = new BlockParams();
1303 |         p.setGenerateEntities(entities);
1304 |         if (position == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
1305 |             Size2D size = t.arrange(g2, constraint);
1306 |             titleArea = createAlignedRectangle2D(size, area, 
1307 |                     t.getHorizontalAlignment(), VerticalAlignment.TOP);
1308 |             retValue = t.draw(g2, titleArea, p);
1309 |             area.setRect(area.getX(), Math.min(area.getY() + size.height, 
1310 |                     area.getMaxY()), area.getWidth(), Math.max(area.getHeight()
1311 |                     - size.height, 0));
1312 |         }
1313 |         else if (position == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
1314 |             Size2D size = t.arrange(g2, constraint);
1315 |             titleArea = createAlignedRectangle2D(size, area, 
1316 |                     t.getHorizontalAlignment(), VerticalAlignment.BOTTOM);
1317 |             retValue = t.draw(g2, titleArea, p);
1318 |             area.setRect(area.getX(), area.getY(), area.getWidth(), 
1319 |                     area.getHeight() - size.height);
1320 |         }
1321 |         else if (position == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
1322 |             Size2D size = t.arrange(g2, constraint);
1323 |             titleArea = createAlignedRectangle2D(size, area, 
1324 |                     HorizontalAlignment.RIGHT, t.getVerticalAlignment());
1325 |             retValue = t.draw(g2, titleArea, p);
1326 |             area.setRect(area.getX(), area.getY(), area.getWidth() 
1327 |                     - size.width, area.getHeight());
1328 |         }
1329 | 
1330 |         else if (position == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
1331 |             Size2D size = t.arrange(g2, constraint);
1332 |             titleArea = createAlignedRectangle2D(size, area, 
1333 |                     HorizontalAlignment.LEFT, t.getVerticalAlignment());
1334 |             retValue = t.draw(g2, titleArea, p);
1335 |             area.setRect(area.getX() + size.width, area.getY(), area.getWidth() 
1336 |                     - size.width, area.getHeight());
1337 |         }
1338 |         else {
1339 |             throw new RuntimeException("Unrecognised title position.");
1340 |         }
1341 |         EntityCollection result = null;
1342 |         if (retValue instanceof EntityBlockResult) {
1343 |             EntityBlockResult ebr = (EntityBlockResult) retValue;
1344 |             result = ebr.getEntityCollection();
1345 |         }
1346 |         return result;   
1347 |     }

629 |         Paint seriesPaint = this.paintList.getPaint(series);
630 |         if (seriesPaint == null && this.autoPopulateSeriesPaint) {
631 |             DrawingSupplier supplier = getDrawingSupplier();
632 |             if (supplier != null) {
633 |                 seriesPaint = supplier.getNextPaint();
634 |                 this.paintList.setPaint(series, seriesPaint);
635 |             }
636 |         }
637 |         if (seriesPaint == null) {
638 |             seriesPaint = this.basePaint;
639 |         }
640 |         return seriesPaint;
641 | 
642 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.RectangleInsets.createOutsetRectangle(Rectangle2D,boolean,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/RectangleInsets.java
Original range: 324-348
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates an outset rectangle.
     * 
     * @param base  the base rectangle (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param horizontal  apply horizontal insets?
     * @param vertical  apply vertical insets? 
     * 
     * @return An outset rectangle.
     */
Method code:
324 |     public Rectangle2D createOutsetRectangle(Rectangle2D base,
325 |                                              boolean horizontal, 
326 |                                              boolean vertical) {
327 |         if (base == null) {
328 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'base' argument.");
329 |         }
330 |         double topMargin = 0.0;
331 |         double bottomMargin = 0.0;
332 |         if (vertical) {
333 |             topMargin = calculateTopOutset(base.getHeight());
334 |             bottomMargin = calculateBottomOutset(base.getHeight());
335 |         }
336 |         double leftMargin = 0.0;
337 |         double rightMargin = 0.0;
338 |         if (horizontal) {
339 |             leftMargin = calculateLeftOutset(base.getWidth());
340 |             rightMargin = calculateRightOutset(base.getWidth());
341 |         }
342 |         return new Rectangle2D.Double(
343 |             base.getX() - leftMargin, 
344 |             base.getY() - topMargin,
345 |             base.getWidth() + leftMargin + rightMargin,
346 |             base.getHeight() + topMargin + bottomMargin
347 |         );
348 |     }

===== M0128 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.Range.combine(Range,Range)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/Range.java
Original range: 198-214
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new range by combining two existing ranges.
     * <P>
     * Note that:
     * <ul>
     *   <li>either range can be <code>null</code>, in which case the other 
     *       range is returned;</li>
     *   <li>if both ranges are <code>null</code> the return value is 
     *       <code>null</code>.</li>
     * </ul>
     *
     * @param range1  the first range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param range2  the second range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     *
     * @return A new range (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
198 |     public static Range combine(Range range1, Range range2) {
199 |         if (range1 == null) {
200 |             return range2;
201 |         }
202 |         else {
203 |             if (range2 == null) {
204 |                 return range1;
205 |             }
206 |             else {
207 |                 double l = Math.min(range1.getLowerBound(), 
208 |                         range2.getLowerBound());
209 |                 double u = Math.max(range1.getUpperBound(), 
210 |                         range2.getUpperBound());
211 |                 return new Range(l, u);
212 |             }
213 |         }
214 |     }

===== M0161 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextBlock.calculateDimensions(Graphics2D)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextBlock.java
Original range: 163-174
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the width and height of the text block.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The width and height.
     */
Method code:
163 |     public Size2D calculateDimensions(Graphics2D g2) {
164 |         double width = 0.0;
165 |         double height = 0.0;
166 |         Iterator iterator = this.lines.iterator();
167 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
168 |             TextLine line = (TextLine) iterator.next();
169 |             Size2D dimension = line.calculateDimensions(g2);
170 |             width = Math.max(width, dimension.getWidth());
171 |             height = height + dimension.getHeight();
172 |         }
173 |         return new Size2D(width, height);
174 |     }

===== M0167 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.renderer.category.StatisticalBarRenderer.drawVerticalItem(Graphics2D,CategoryItemRendererState,Rectangle2D,CategoryPlot,CategoryAxis,ValueAxis,StatisticalCategoryDataset,int,int)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/renderer/category/StatisticalBarRenderer.java
Original range: 373-500
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws an item for a plot with a vertical orientation.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param state  the renderer state.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param plot  the plot.
     * @param domainAxis  the domain axis.
     * @param rangeAxis  the range axis.
     * @param dataset  the data.
     * @param row  the row index (zero-based).
     * @param column  the column index (zero-based).
     */
Method code:
373 |     protected void drawVerticalItem(Graphics2D g2,
374 |                                     CategoryItemRendererState state,
375 |                                     Rectangle2D dataArea,
376 |                                     CategoryPlot plot,
377 |                                     CategoryAxis domainAxis,
378 |                                     ValueAxis rangeAxis,
379 |                                     StatisticalCategoryDataset dataset,
380 |                                     int row,
381 |                                     int column) {
382 |                                      
383 |         RectangleEdge xAxisLocation = plot.getDomainAxisEdge();
384 |         
385 |         // BAR X
386 |         double rectX = domainAxis.getCategoryStart(
387 |             column, getColumnCount(), dataArea, xAxisLocation
388 |         );
389 | 
390 |         int seriesCount = getRowCount();
391 |         int categoryCount = getColumnCount();
392 |         if (seriesCount > 1) {
393 |             double seriesGap = dataArea.getWidth() * getItemMargin()
394 |                                / (categoryCount * (seriesCount - 1));
395 |             rectX = rectX + row * (state.getBarWidth() + seriesGap);
396 |         }
397 |         else {
398 |             rectX = rectX + row * state.getBarWidth();
399 |         }
400 | 
401 |         // BAR Y
402 |         Number meanValue = dataset.getMeanValue(row, column);
403 | 
404 |         double value = meanValue.doubleValue();
405 |         double base = 0.0;
406 |         double lclip = getLowerClip();
407 |         double uclip = getUpperClip();
408 | 
409 |         if (uclip <= 0.0) {  // cases 1, 2, 3 and 4
410 |             if (value >= uclip) {
411 |                 return; // bar is not visible
412 |             }
413 |             base = uclip;
414 |             if (value <= lclip) {
415 |                 value = lclip;
416 |             }
417 |         }
418 |         else if (lclip <= 0.0) { // cases 5, 6, 7 and 8
419 |             if (value >= uclip) {
420 |                 value = uclip;
421 |             }
422 |             else {
423 |                 if (value <= lclip) {
424 |                     value = lclip;
425 |                 }
426 |             }
427 |         }
428 |         else { // cases 9, 10, 11 and 12
429 |             if (value <= lclip) {
430 |                 return; // bar is not visible
431 |             }
432 |             base = getLowerClip();
433 |             if (value >= uclip) {
434 |                value = uclip;
435 |             }
436 |         }
437 | 
438 |         RectangleEdge yAxisLocation = plot.getRangeAxisEdge();
439 |         double transY1 = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(base, dataArea, yAxisLocation);
440 |         double transY2 = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(value, dataArea, 
441 |                 yAxisLocation);
442 |         double rectY = Math.min(transY2, transY1);
443 | 
444 |         double rectWidth = state.getBarWidth();
445 |         double rectHeight = Math.abs(transY2 - transY1);
446 | 
447 |         Rectangle2D bar = new Rectangle2D.Double(rectX, rectY, rectWidth, 
448 |                 rectHeight);
449 |         Paint seriesPaint = getItemPaint(row, column);
450 |         g2.setPaint(seriesPaint);
451 |         g2.fill(bar);
452 |         if (isDrawBarOutline() && state.getBarWidth() > 3) {
453 |             g2.setStroke(getItemStroke(row, column));
454 |             g2.setPaint(getItemOutlinePaint(row, column));
455 |             g2.draw(bar);
456 |         }
457 | 
458 |         // standard deviation lines
459 |             double valueDelta = dataset.getStdDevValue(row, column).doubleValue();
460 |             double highVal = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(meanValue.doubleValue() 
461 |                     + valueDelta, dataArea, yAxisLocation);
462 |             double lowVal = rangeAxis.valueToJava2D(meanValue.doubleValue() 
463 |                     - valueDelta, dataArea, yAxisLocation);
464 | 
465 |             if (this.errorIndicatorStroke != null) {
466 |                 g2.setStroke(this.errorIndicatorStroke);
467 |             }
468 |             else {
469 |                 g2.setStroke(getItemOutlineStroke(row, column));
470 |             }
471 |             if (this.errorIndicatorPaint != null) {
472 |                 g2.setPaint(this.errorIndicatorPaint);  
473 |             }
474 |             else {
475 |                 g2.setPaint(getItemOutlinePaint(row, column));   
476 |             }
477 |             Line2D line = null;
478 |             line = new Line2D.Double(rectX + rectWidth / 2.0d, lowVal,
479 |                                      rectX + rectWidth / 2.0d, highVal);
480 |             g2.draw(line);
481 |             line = new Line2D.Double(rectX + rectWidth / 2.0d - 5.0d, highVal,
482 |                                      rectX + rectWidth / 2.0d + 5.0d, highVal);
483 |             g2.draw(line);
484 |             line = new Line2D.Double(rectX + rectWidth / 2.0d - 5.0d, lowVal,
485 |                                      rectX + rectWidth / 2.0d + 5.0d, lowVal);
486 |             g2.draw(line);
487 |         
488 |         CategoryItemLabelGenerator generator = getItemLabelGenerator(row, 
489 |                 column);
490 |         if (generator != null && isItemLabelVisible(row, column)) {
491 |             drawItemLabel(g2, dataset, row, column, plot, generator, bar, 
492 |                     (value < 0.0));
493 |         }        
494 | 
495 |         // add an item entity, if this information is being collected
496 |         EntityCollection entities = state.getEntityCollection();
497 |         if (entities != null) {
498 |             addItemEntity(entities, dataset, row, column, bar);
499 |         }
500 |     }

Method code:

178 |     public Size2D calculateDimensions(Graphics2D g2) {

/**
     * Draws a grid line against the range axis.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param plot  the plot.
     * @param axis  the value axis.
     * @param dataArea  the area for plotting data (not yet adjusted for any
     *                  3D effect).

Method code:
868 |     public void drawRangeGridline(Graphics2D g2,
869 |                                   CategoryPlot plot,
870 |                                   ValueAxis axis,
871 |                                   Rectangle2D dataArea,
872 |                                   double value) {
873 | 
874 |         Range range = axis.getRange();
875 |         if (!range.contains(value)) {
876 |             return;
877 |         }
878 | 
879 |         PlotOrientation orientation = plot.getOrientation();
880 |         double v = axis.valueToJava2D(value, dataArea, plot.getRangeAxisEdge());
881 |         Line2D line = null;
882 |         if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
883 |             line = new Line2D.Double(v, dataArea.getMinY(), v,
884 |                     dataArea.getMaxY());
885 |         }
886 |         else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
887 |             line = new Line2D.Double(dataArea.getMinX(), v,
888 |                     dataArea.getMaxX(), v);
889 |         }
890 | 
891 |         Paint paint = plot.getRangeGridlinePaint();
892 |         if (paint == null) {
893 |             paint = CategoryPlot.DEFAULT_GRIDLINE_PAINT;
894 |         }
895 |         g2.setPaint(paint);
896 | 
897 |         Stroke stroke = plot.getRangeGridlineStroke();
898 |         if (stroke == null) {
899 |             stroke = CategoryPlot.DEFAULT_GRIDLINE_STROKE;
900 |         }
901 |         g2.setStroke(stroke);
902 | 
903 |         g2.draw(line);
904 | 
905 |     }

===== M0197 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.refreshTicks(Graphics2D,AxisState,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 968-1029
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a temporary list of ticks that can be used when drawing the axis.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (used to get font measurements).
     * @param state  the axis state.
     * @param dataArea  the area inside the axes.
     * @param edge  the location of the axis.
     * 
     * @return A list of ticks.
     */
Method code:
968 |     public List refreshTicks(Graphics2D g2, 
969 |                              AxisState state,
970 |                              Rectangle2D dataArea,
971 |                              RectangleEdge edge) {
972 | 
973 |         List ticks = new java.util.ArrayList();
974 |         
975 |         // sanity check for data area...
976 |         if (dataArea.getHeight() <= 0.0 || dataArea.getWidth() < 0.0) {
977 |             return ticks;
978 |         }
979 | 
980 |         CategoryPlot plot = (CategoryPlot) getPlot();
981 |         List categories = plot.getCategoriesForAxis(this);
982 |         double max = 0.0;
983 |                 
984 |         if (categories != null) {
985 |             CategoryLabelPosition position 
986 |                     = this.categoryLabelPositions.getLabelPosition(edge);
987 |             float r = this.maximumCategoryLabelWidthRatio;
988 |             if (r <= 0.0) {
989 |                 r = position.getWidthRatio();   
990 |             }
991 |                   
992 |             float l = 0.0f;
993 |             if (position.getWidthType() == CategoryLabelWidthType.CATEGORY) {
994 |                 l = (float) calculateCategorySize(categories.size(), dataArea, 
995 |                         edge);  
996 |             }
997 |             else {
998 |                 if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
999 |                     l = (float) dataArea.getWidth();   
1000 |                 }
1001 |                 else {
1002 |                     l = (float) dataArea.getHeight();   
1003 |                 }
1004 |             }
1005 |             int categoryIndex = 0;
1006 |             Iterator iterator = categories.iterator();
1007 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1008 |                 Comparable category = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1009 |                 TextBlock label = createLabel(category, l * r, edge, g2);
1010 |                 if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP || edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
1011 |                     max = Math.max(max, calculateTextBlockHeight(label, 
1012 |                             position, g2));
1013 |                 }
1014 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT 
1015 |                         || edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
1016 |                     max = Math.max(max, calculateTextBlockWidth(label, 
1017 |                             position, g2));
1018 |                 }
1019 |                 Tick tick = new CategoryTick(category, label, 
1020 |                         position.getLabelAnchor(), position.getRotationAnchor(), 
1021 |                         position.getAngle());
1022 |                 ticks.add(tick);
1023 |                 categoryIndex = categoryIndex + 1;
1024 |             }
1025 |         }
1026 |         state.setMax(max);
1027 |         return ticks;
1028 |         
1029 |     }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.CategoryPlot.getDataRange(ValueAxis)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/CategoryPlot.java
Original range: 3137-3162
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range of data values that will be plotted against the range 
     * axis.  If the dataset is <code>null</code>, this method returns 
     * <code>null</code>.
     *
     * @param axis  the axis.
     *
     * @return The data range.
     */
Method code:
3137 |     public Range getDataRange(ValueAxis axis) {
3138 | 
3139 |         Range result = null;
3140 |         List mappedDatasets = new ArrayList();
3141 |         
3142 |         int rangeIndex = this.rangeAxes.indexOf(axis);
3143 |         if (rangeIndex >= 0) {
3144 |             mappedDatasets.addAll(datasetsMappedToRangeAxis(rangeIndex));
3145 |         }
3146 |         else if (axis == getRangeAxis()) {
3147 |             mappedDatasets.addAll(datasetsMappedToRangeAxis(0));
3148 |         }
3149 | 
3150 |         // iterate through the datasets that map to the axis and get the union 
3151 |         // of the ranges.
3152 |         Iterator iterator = mappedDatasets.iterator();
3153 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
3154 |             CategoryDataset d = (CategoryDataset) iterator.next();
3155 |             CategoryItemRenderer r = getRendererForDataset(d);
3156 |             if (r != null) {
3157 |                 result = Range.combine(result, r.findRangeBounds(d));
3158 |             }
3159 |         }
3160 |         return result;
3161 | 
3162 |     }

Original range: 332-347
Documentation:
/**
     * Translates a shape to a new location such that the anchor point 
     * (relative to the rectangular bounds of the shape) aligns with the 
     * specified (x, y) coordinate in Java2D space.
     *  
     * @param shape  the shape (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param anchor  the anchor (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param locationX  the x-coordinate (in Java2D space).
     * @param locationY  the y-coordinate (in Java2D space).
     * 
     * @return A new and translated shape.
     */
Method code:
332 |     public static Shape createTranslatedShape(Shape shape, 
333 |                                               RectangleAnchor anchor, 
334 |                                               double locationX,
335 |                                               double locationY) {
336 |         if (shape == null) {
337 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'shape' argument.");
338 |         }        
339 |         if (anchor == null) {
340 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'anchor' argument.");
341 |         }
342 |         Point2D anchorPoint = RectangleAnchor.coordinates(shape.getBounds2D(), 
343 |                 anchor);
344 |         AffineTransform transform = AffineTransform.getTranslateInstance(
345 |                 locationX - anchorPoint.getX(), locationY - anchorPoint.getY());
346 |         return transform.createTransformedShape(shape);   
347 |     }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextFragment.calculateBaselineOffset(Graphics2D,TextAnchor)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextFragment.java
Original range: 238-252
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the vertical offset between the baseline and the specified 
     * text anchor.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param anchor  the anchor.
     * 
     * @return the offset.
     */
Method code:
238 |     public float calculateBaselineOffset(Graphics2D g2, TextAnchor anchor) {
239 |         float result = 0.0f;
240 |         FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(this.font);
241 |         LineMetrics lm = fm.getLineMetrics("ABCxyz", g2);
242 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
243 |                                           || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT) {
244 |             result = lm.getAscent();
245 |         }
246 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT 
247 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
248 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
249 |             result = -lm.getDescent() - lm.getLeading();
250 |         }
251 |         return result;                                             
252 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.JFreeChart.createAlignedRectangle2D(Size2D,Rectangle2D,HorizontalAlignment,VerticalAlignment)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/JFreeChart.java
Original range: 1237-1263
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a rectangle that is aligned to the frame.
     * 
     * @param dimensions
     * @param frame
     * @param hAlign
     * @param vAlign
     * 
     * @return A rectangle.
     */
Method code:
1237 |     private Rectangle2D createAlignedRectangle2D(Size2D dimensions, 
1238 |             Rectangle2D frame, HorizontalAlignment hAlign, 
1239 |             VerticalAlignment vAlign) {
1240 |         double x = Double.NaN;
1241 |         double y = Double.NaN;
1242 |         if (hAlign == HorizontalAlignment.LEFT) {
1243 |             x = frame.getX();   
1244 |         }
1245 |         else if (hAlign == HorizontalAlignment.CENTER) {
1246 |             x = frame.getCenterX() - (dimensions.width / 2.0);   
1247 |         }
1248 |         else if (hAlign == HorizontalAlignment.RIGHT) {
1249 |             x = frame.getMaxX() - dimensions.width;   
1250 |         }
1251 |         if (vAlign == VerticalAlignment.TOP) {
1252 |             y = frame.getY();   
1253 |         }
1254 |         else if (vAlign == VerticalAlignment.CENTER) {
1255 |             y = frame.getCenterY() - (dimensions.height / 2.0);   
1256 |         }
1257 |         else if (vAlign == VerticalAlignment.BOTTOM) {
1258 |             y = frame.getMaxY() - dimensions.height;   
1259 |         }
1260 |         
1261 |         return new Rectangle2D.Double(x, y, dimensions.width, 
1262 |                 dimensions.height);
1263 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.data.KeyedObjects.getIndex(Comparable)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/KeyedObjects.java
Original range: 125-137
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the index for a given key.
     *
     * @param key  the key.
     *
     * @return The index, or <code>-1</code> if the key is unrecognised.
     */
Method code:
125 |     public int getIndex(Comparable key) {
126 |         int result = -1;
127 |         int i = 0;
128 |         Iterator iterator = this.data.iterator();
129 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
130 |             KeyedObject ko = (KeyedObject) iterator.next();
131 |             if (ko.getKey().equals(key)) {
132 |                 result = i;
133 |             }
134 |             i++;
135 |         }
136 |         return result;
137 |     }

===== M0231 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.reserveSpace(Graphics2D,Plot,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge,AxisSpace)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 731-783
Documentation:
/**
     * Estimates the space required for the axis, given a specific drawing area.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (used to obtain font information).
     * @param plot  the plot that the axis belongs to.
     * @param plotArea  the area within which the axis should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location (top or bottom).
     * @param space  the space already reserved.
     *
     * @return The space required to draw the axis.
     */
Method code:
731 |     public AxisSpace reserveSpace(Graphics2D g2, Plot plot, 
732 |                                   Rectangle2D plotArea, 
733 |                                   RectangleEdge edge, AxisSpace space) {
734 | 
735 |         // create a new space object if one wasn't supplied...
736 |         if (space == null) {
737 |             space = new AxisSpace();
738 |         }
739 |         
740 |         // if the axis is not visible, no additional space is required...
741 |         if (!isVisible()) {
742 |             return space;
743 |         }
744 | 
745 |         // calculate the max size of the tick labels (if visible)...
746 |         double tickLabelHeight = 0.0;
747 |         double tickLabelWidth = 0.0;
748 |         if (isTickLabelsVisible()) {
749 |             g2.setFont(getTickLabelFont());
750 |             AxisState state = new AxisState();
751 |             // we call refresh ticks just to get the maximum width or height
752 |             refreshTicks(g2, state, plotArea, edge);
753 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
754 |                 tickLabelHeight = state.getMax();
755 |             }
756 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
757 |                 tickLabelHeight = state.getMax();
758 |             }
759 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
760 |                 tickLabelWidth = state.getMax(); 
761 |             }
762 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
763 |                 tickLabelWidth = state.getMax(); 
764 |             }
765 |         }
766 |         
767 |         // get the axis label size and update the space object...
768 |         Rectangle2D labelEnclosure = getLabelEnclosure(g2, edge);
769 |         double labelHeight = 0.0;
770 |         double labelWidth = 0.0;
771 |         if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
772 |             labelHeight = labelEnclosure.getHeight();
773 |             space.add(labelHeight + tickLabelHeight 
774 |                     + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset, edge);
775 |         }
776 |         else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
777 |             labelWidth = labelEnclosure.getWidth();
778 |             space.add(labelWidth + tickLabelWidth 
779 |                     + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset, edge);
780 |         }
781 |         return space;
782 | 
783 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the tick unit in the collection that is greater than or equal
     * to (in size) the specified unit.
     *
     * @param unit  the unit.
     *
     * @return A unit from the collection.
     */
Method code:
153 |     public TickUnit getCeilingTickUnit(TickUnit unit) {
154 | 
155 |         int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.tickUnits, unit);
156 |         if (index >= 0) {
157 |             return (TickUnit) this.tickUnits.get(index);
158 |         }
159 |         else {
160 |             index = -(index + 1);
161 |             return (TickUnit) this.tickUnits.get(Math.min(index, 
162 |                     this.tickUnits.size() - 1));
163 |         }
164 | 
165 |     }

===== M0245 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.renderer.category.AbstractCategoryItemRenderer.drawRangeMarker(Graphics2D,CategoryPlot,ValueAxis,Marker,Rectangle2D)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/renderer/category/AbstractCategoryItemRenderer.java
Original range: 1005-1159
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws a marker for the range axis.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (not <code>null</code>).
     * @param plot  the plot (not <code>null</code>).
     * @param axis  the range axis (not <code>null</code>).
     * @param marker  the marker to be drawn (not <code>null</code>).
     * @param dataArea  the area inside the axes (not <code>null</code>).
     *
     * @see #drawDomainMarker(Graphics2D, CategoryPlot, CategoryAxis,
     *     CategoryMarker, Rectangle2D)
     */
Method code:
1005 |     public void drawRangeMarker(Graphics2D g2,
1006 |                                 CategoryPlot plot,
1007 |                                 ValueAxis axis,
1008 |                                 Marker marker,
1009 |                                 Rectangle2D dataArea) {
1010 | 
1011 |         if (marker instanceof ValueMarker) {
1012 |             ValueMarker vm = (ValueMarker) marker;
1013 |             double value = vm.getValue();
1014 |             Range range = axis.getRange();
1015 | 
1016 |             if (!range.contains(value)) {
1017 |                 return;
1018 |             }
1019 | 
1020 |             final Composite savedComposite = g2.getComposite();
1021 |             g2.setComposite(AlphaComposite.getInstance(
1022 |                     AlphaComposite.SRC_OVER, marker.getAlpha()));
1023 | 
1024 |             PlotOrientation orientation = plot.getOrientation();
1025 |             double v = axis.valueToJava2D(value, dataArea,
1026 |                     plot.getRangeAxisEdge());
1027 |             Line2D line = null;
1028 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1029 |                 line = new Line2D.Double(v, dataArea.getMinY(), v,
1030 |                         dataArea.getMaxY());
1031 |             }
1032 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1033 |                 line = new Line2D.Double(dataArea.getMinX(), v,
1034 |                         dataArea.getMaxX(), v);
1035 |             }
1036 | 
1037 |             g2.setPaint(marker.getPaint());
1038 |             g2.setStroke(marker.getStroke());
1039 |             g2.draw(line);
1040 | 
1041 |             String label = marker.getLabel();
1042 |             RectangleAnchor anchor = marker.getLabelAnchor();
1043 |             if (label != null) {
1044 |                 Font labelFont = marker.getLabelFont();
1045 |                 g2.setFont(labelFont);
1046 |                 g2.setPaint(marker.getLabelPaint());
1047 |                 Point2D coordinates = calculateRangeMarkerTextAnchorPoint(
1048 |                         g2, orientation, dataArea, line.getBounds2D(),
1049 |                         marker.getLabelOffset(), LengthAdjustmentType.EXPAND,
1050 |                         anchor);
1051 |                 TextUtilities.drawAlignedString(label, g2,
1052 |                         (float) coordinates.getX(), (float) coordinates.getY(),
1053 |                         marker.getLabelTextAnchor());
1054 |             }
1055 |             g2.setComposite(savedComposite);
1056 |         }
1057 |         else if (marker instanceof IntervalMarker) {
1058 |             IntervalMarker im = (IntervalMarker) marker;
1059 |             double start = im.getStartValue();
1060 |             double end = im.getEndValue();
1061 |             Range range = axis.getRange();
1062 |             if (!(range.intersects(start, end))) {
1063 |                 return;
1064 |             }
1065 | 
1066 |             final Composite savedComposite = g2.getComposite();
1067 |             g2.setComposite(AlphaComposite.getInstance(
1068 |                     AlphaComposite.SRC_OVER, marker.getAlpha()));
1069 | 
1070 |             double start2d = axis.valueToJava2D(start, dataArea,
1071 |                     plot.getRangeAxisEdge());
1072 |             double end2d = axis.valueToJava2D(end, dataArea,
1073 |                     plot.getRangeAxisEdge());
1074 |             double low = Math.min(start2d, end2d);
1075 |             double high = Math.max(start2d, end2d);
1076 | 
1077 |             PlotOrientation orientation = plot.getOrientation();
1078 |             Rectangle2D rect = null;
1079 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1080 |                 // clip left and right bounds to data area
1081 |                 low = Math.max(low, dataArea.getMinX());
1082 |                 high = Math.min(high, dataArea.getMaxX());
1083 |                 rect = new Rectangle2D.Double(low,
1084 |                         dataArea.getMinY(), high - low,
1085 |                         dataArea.getHeight());
1086 |             }
1087 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1088 |                 // clip top and bottom bounds to data area
1089 |                 low = Math.max(low, dataArea.getMinY());
1090 |                 high = Math.min(high, dataArea.getMaxY());
1091 |                 rect = new Rectangle2D.Double(dataArea.getMinX(),
1092 |                         low, dataArea.getWidth(),
1093 |                         high - low);
1094 |             }
1095 |             Paint p = marker.getPaint();
1096 |             if (p instanceof GradientPaint) {
1097 |                 GradientPaint gp = (GradientPaint) p;
1098 |                 GradientPaintTransformer t = im.getGradientPaintTransformer();
1099 |                 if (t != null) {
1100 |                     gp = t.transform(gp, rect);
1101 |                 }
1102 |                 g2.setPaint(gp);
1103 |             }
1104 |             else {
1105 |                 g2.setPaint(p);
1106 |             }
1107 |             g2.fill(rect);
1108 | 
1109 |             // now draw the outlines, if visible...
1110 |             if (im.getOutlinePaint() != null && im.getOutlineStroke() != null) {
1111 |                 if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1112 |                     Line2D line = new Line2D.Double();
1113 |                     double x0 = dataArea.getMinX();
1114 |                     double x1 = dataArea.getMaxX();
1115 |                     g2.setPaint(im.getOutlinePaint());
1116 |                     g2.setStroke(im.getOutlineStroke());
1117 |                     if (range.contains(start)) {
1118 |                         line.setLine(x0, start2d, x1, start2d);
1119 |                         g2.draw(line);
1120 |                     }
1121 |                     if (range.contains(end)) {
1122 |                         line.setLine(x0, end2d, x1, end2d);
1123 |                         g2.draw(line);
1124 |                     }
1125 |                 }
1126 |                 else { // PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL
1127 |                     Line2D line = new Line2D.Double();
1128 |                     double y0 = dataArea.getMinY();
1129 |                     double y1 = dataArea.getMaxY();
1130 |                     g2.setPaint(im.getOutlinePaint());
1131 |                     g2.setStroke(im.getOutlineStroke());
1132 |                     if (range.contains(start)) {
1133 |                         line.setLine(start2d, y0, start2d, y1);
1134 |                         g2.draw(line);
1135 |                     }
1136 |                     if (range.contains(end)) {
1137 |                         line.setLine(end2d, y0, end2d, y1);
1138 |                         g2.draw(line);
1139 |                     }
1140 |                 }
1141 |             }
1142 | 
1143 |             String label = marker.getLabel();
1144 |             RectangleAnchor anchor = marker.getLabelAnchor();
1145 |             if (label != null) {
1146 |                 Font labelFont = marker.getLabelFont();
1147 |                 g2.setFont(labelFont);
1148 |                 g2.setPaint(marker.getLabelPaint());
1149 |                 Point2D coordinates = calculateRangeMarkerTextAnchorPoint(
1150 |                         g2, orientation, dataArea, rect,
1151 |                         marker.getLabelOffset(), marker.getLabelOffsetType(),
1152 |                         anchor);
1153 |                 TextUtilities.drawAlignedString(label, g2,
1154 |                         (float) coordinates.getX(), (float) coordinates.getY(),
1155 |                         marker.getLabelTextAnchor());
1156 |             }
1157 |             g2.setComposite(savedComposite);
1158 |         }
1159 |     }
