Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.FlowArrangement.arrange(BlockContainer,Graphics2D,RectangleConstraint)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/FlowArrangement.java
Original range: 126-166
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates and sets the bounds of all the items in the specified 
     * container, subject to the given constraint.  The <code>Graphics2D</code>
     * can be used by some items (particularly items containing text) to 
     * calculate sizing parameters.
     * 
     * @param container  the container whose items are being arranged.
     * @param constraint  the size constraint.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The size of the container after arrangement of the contents.
     */
Method code:
126 |     public Size2D arrange(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2,
127 |                           RectangleConstraint constraint) {
128 |         
129 |         LengthConstraintType w = constraint.getWidthConstraintType();
130 |         LengthConstraintType h = constraint.getHeightConstraintType();
131 |         if (w == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
132 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
133 |                 return arrangeNN(container, g2);  
134 |             }
135 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
136 |                 return arrangeNF(container, g2, constraint);  
137 |             }
138 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
139 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
140 |             }
141 |         }
142 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
143 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
144 |                 return arrangeFN(container, g2, constraint);  
145 |             }
146 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
147 |                 return arrangeFF(container, g2, constraint);  
148 |             }
149 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
150 |                 return arrangeFR(container, g2, constraint);  
151 |             }
152 |         }
153 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
154 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
155 |                 return arrangeRN(container, g2, constraint);  
156 |             }
157 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
158 |                 return arrangeRF(container, g2, constraint);  
159 |             }
160 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
161 |                 return arrangeRR(container, g2, constraint);   
162 |             }
163 |         }
164 |         throw new RuntimeException("Unrecognised constraint type.");
165 |         
166 |     }

===== M0268 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.ObjectUtilities.getClassLoader(Class)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/ObjectUtilities.java
Original range: 256-276
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the classloader, which was responsible for loading the given
     * class.
     *
     * @param c the classloader, either an application class loader or the
     *          boot loader.
     * @return the classloader, never null.
     * @throws SecurityException if the SecurityManager does not allow to grab
     *                           the context classloader.
     */
Method code:
256 |     public synchronized static ClassLoader getClassLoader(final Class c) {
257 |         if (classLoader != null) {
258 |             return classLoader;
259 |         }
260 |         if ("ThreadContext".equals(classLoaderSource)) {
261 |             final ClassLoader threadLoader
262 |                 = Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader();
263 |             if (threadLoader != null) {
264 |                 return threadLoader;
265 |             }
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         // Context classloader - do not cache ..
269 |         final ClassLoader applicationCL = c.getClassLoader();
270 |         if (applicationCL == null) {
271 |             return ClassLoader.getSystemClassLoader();
272 |         }
273 |         else {
274 |             return applicationCL;
275 |         }
276 |     }

/**
     * Returns a legend item for a series.
     *
     * @param datasetIndex  the dataset index (zero-based).
     * @param series  the series index (zero-based).
     *
     * @return The legend item (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
657 |     public LegendItem getLegendItem(int datasetIndex, int series) {
658 | 
659 |         CategoryPlot cp = getPlot();
660 |         if (cp == null) {
661 |             return null;
662 |         }
663 | 
664 |         // check that a legend item needs to be displayed...
665 |         if (!isSeriesVisible(series) || !isSeriesVisibleInLegend(series)) {
666 |             return null;
667 |         }
668 |

===== M0276 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.createTextBlock(String,Font,Paint,float,int,TextMeasurer)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 185-223
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new text block from the given string, breaking the
     * text into lines so that the <code>maxWidth</code> value is
     * respected.
     * 
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param font  the font.
     * @param paint  the paint.
     * @param maxWidth  the maximum width for each line.
     * @param maxLines  the maximum number of lines.
     * @param measurer  the text measurer.
     * 
     * @return A text block.
     */
Method code:
185 |     public static TextBlock createTextBlock(String text, Font font,
186 |             Paint paint, float maxWidth, int maxLines, TextMeasurer measurer) {
187 |         
188 |         TextBlock result = new TextBlock();
189 |         BreakIterator iterator = BreakIterator.getLineInstance();
190 |         iterator.setText(text);
191 |         int current = 0;
192 |         int lines = 0;
193 |         int length = text.length();
194 |         while (current < length && lines < maxLines) {
195 |             int next = nextLineBreak(text, current, maxWidth, iterator, 
196 |                     measurer);
197 |             if (next == BreakIterator.DONE) {
198 |                 result.addLine(text.substring(current), font, paint);
199 |                 return result;
200 |             }
201 |             result.addLine(text.substring(current, next), font, paint);
202 |             lines++;
203 |             current = next;
204 |             while (current < text.length()&& text.charAt(current) == '\n') {
205 |                 current++;
206 |             }
207 |         }
208 |         if (current < length) {
209 |             TextLine lastLine = result.getLastLine();
210 |             TextFragment lastFragment = lastLine.getLastTextFragment();
211 |             String oldStr = lastFragment.getText();
212 |             String newStr = "...";
213 |             if (oldStr.length() > 3) {
214 |                 newStr = oldStr.substring(0, oldStr.length() - 3) + "...";
215 |             }
216 | 
217 |             lastLine.removeFragment(lastFragment);
218 |             TextFragment newFragment = new TextFragment(newStr, 
219 |                     lastFragment.getFont(), lastFragment.getPaint());
220 |             lastLine.addFragment(newFragment);
221 |         }
222 |         return result;
223 |     }

===== M0329 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.Axis.getLabelEnclosure(Graphics2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/Axis.java
Original range: 1054-1077
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns a rectangle that encloses the axis label.  This is typically 
     * used for layout purposes (it gives the maximum dimensions of the label).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param edge  the edge of the plot area along which the axis is measuring.
     *
     * @return The enclosing rectangle.
     */
Method code:
1054 |     protected Rectangle2D getLabelEnclosure(Graphics2D g2, RectangleEdge edge) {
1055 | 
1056 |         Rectangle2D result = new Rectangle2D.Double();
1057 |         String axisLabel = getLabel();
1058 |         if (axisLabel != null && !axisLabel.equals("")) {
1059 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(getLabelFont());
1060 |             Rectangle2D bounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(axisLabel, g2, fm);
1061 |             RectangleInsets insets = getLabelInsets();
1062 |             bounds = insets.createOutsetRectangle(bounds);
1063 |             double angle = getLabelAngle();
1064 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT || edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
1065 |                 angle = angle - Math.PI / 2.0;
1066 |             }
1067 |             double x = bounds.getCenterX();
1068 |             double y = bounds.getCenterY();
1069 |             AffineTransform transformer 
1070 |                 = AffineTransform.getRotateInstance(angle, x, y);
1071 |             Shape labelBounds = transformer.createTransformedShape(bounds);
1072 |             result = labelBounds.getBounds2D();
1073 |         }
1074 | 
1075 |         return result;
1076 | 
1077 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.Marker.Marker(Paint,Stroke,Paint,Stroke,float)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/Marker.java
Original range: 172-200
Documentation:
/**
     * Constructs a new marker.
     *
     * @param paint  the paint (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param stroke  the stroke (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param outlinePaint  the outline paint (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param outlineStroke  the outline stroke (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param alpha  the alpha transparency (must be in the range 0.0f to 
     *     1.0f).
     *     
     * @throws IllegalArgumentException if <code>paint</code> or 
     *     <code>stroke</code> is <code>null</code>, or <code>alpha</code> is 
     *     not in the specified range.
     */
Method code:
172 |     protected Marker(Paint paint, Stroke stroke, 
173 |                      Paint outlinePaint, Stroke outlineStroke, 
174 |                      float alpha) {
175 | 
176 |         if (paint == null) {
177 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'paint' argument.");
178 |         }
179 |         if (stroke == null) {
180 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'stroke' argument.");
181 |         }
182 |         if (alpha < 0.0f || alpha > 1.0f)
183 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
184 |                     "The 'alpha' value must be in the range 0.0f to 1.0f");
185 |         
186 |         this.paint = paint;
187 |         this.stroke = stroke;

/**
     * Fills the specified area with the background paint.  If the background
     * paint is an instance of <code>GradientPaint</code>, the gradient will
     * run in the direction suggested by the plot's orientation.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics target.
     * @param area  the plot area.
     * @param orientation  the plot orientation (<code>null</code> not 
     *         permitted).
     * 
     * @since 1.0.6
     */

963 |     protected void fillBackground(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area,

967 |         }
968 |         if (this.backgroundPaint == null) {
969 |             return;
970 |         }

973 |             GradientPaint gp = (GradientPaint) p;
974 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
975 |                 p = new GradientPaint((float) area.getCenterX(), 
976 |                         (float) area.getMaxY(), gp.getColor1(), 
977 |                         (float) area.getCenterX(), (float) area.getMinY(), 
978 |                         gp.getColor2());
979 |             }
980 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
981 |                 p = new GradientPaint((float) area.getMinX(), 
982 |                         (float) area.getCenterY(), gp.getColor1(), 
983 |                         (float) area.getMaxX(), (float) area.getCenterY(), 
984 |                         gp.getColor2());
985 |             }
986 |         }

988 |         g2.setComposite(AlphaComposite.getInstance(AlphaComposite.SRC_OVER,
989 |                 this.backgroundAlpha));
990 |         g2.setPaint(p);
991 |         g2.fill(area);

935 |     public Paint lookupSeriesOutlinePaint(int series) {

===== M0367 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.Plot.resolveRangeAxisLocation(AxisLocation,PlotOrientation)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/Plot.java
Original range: 1365-1416
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolves a range axis location for a given plot orientation.
     *
     * @param location  the location (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param orientation  the orientation (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The edge (never <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
1365 |     public static RectangleEdge resolveRangeAxisLocation(
1366 |             AxisLocation location, PlotOrientation orientation) {
1367 | 
1368 |         if (location == null) {
1369 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'location' argument.");   
1370 |         }
1371 |         if (orientation == null) {
1372 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'orientation' argument.");
1373 |         }
1374 | 
1375 |         RectangleEdge result = null;
1376 |         
1377 |         if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_RIGHT) {
1378 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1379 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1380 |             }
1381 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1382 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1383 |             }
1384 |         }
1385 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_LEFT) {
1386 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1387 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1388 |             }
1389 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1390 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1391 |             }
1392 |         }
1393 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_RIGHT) {
1394 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1395 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1396 |             }
1397 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1398 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1399 |             }
1400 |         }
1401 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_LEFT) {
1402 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1403 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1404 |             }
1405 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1406 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1407 |             }
1408 |         }
1409 | 
1410 |         // the above should cover all the options...
1411 |         if (result == null) {
1412 |             throw new IllegalStateException("resolveRangeAxisLocation()");
1413 |         }
1414 |         return result;
1415 |         
1416 |     }

===== M0368 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.BorderArrangement.arrange(BlockContainer,Graphics2D,RectangleConstraint)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/BorderArrangement.java
Original range: 128-176
Documentation:
/**
     * Arranges the items in the specified container, subject to the given 
     * constraint.
     * 
     * @param container  the container.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param constraint  the constraint.
     * 
     * @return The block size.
     */
Method code:
128 |     public Size2D arrange(BlockContainer container, 
129 |                           Graphics2D g2, 
130 |                           RectangleConstraint constraint) {
131 |         RectangleConstraint contentConstraint 
132 |             = container.toContentConstraint(constraint);
133 |         Size2D contentSize = null;
134 |         LengthConstraintType w = contentConstraint.getWidthConstraintType();
135 |         LengthConstraintType h = contentConstraint.getHeightConstraintType();
136 |         if (w == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
137 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
138 |                 contentSize = arrangeNN(container, g2);  
139 |             }
140 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
141 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
142 |             }
143 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
144 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
145 |             }
146 |         }
147 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
148 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
149 |                 contentSize = arrangeFN(container, g2, constraint.getWidth());  
150 |             }
151 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
152 |                 contentSize = arrangeFF(container, g2, constraint);  
153 |             }
154 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
155 |                 contentSize = arrangeFR(container, g2, constraint);  
156 |             }
157 |         }
158 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
159 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
160 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
161 |             }
162 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
163 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
164 |             }
165 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
166 |                 contentSize = arrangeRR(
167 |                     container, constraint.getWidthRange(),
168 |                     constraint.getHeightRange(), g2
169 |                 );  
170 |             }
171 |         }
172 |         return new Size2D(
173 |             container.calculateTotalWidth(contentSize.getWidth()),
174 |             container.calculateTotalHeight(contentSize.getHeight())
175 |         );
176 |     }

===== M0382 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.selectHorizontalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 1069-1096
Documentation:
/**
     * Selects an appropriate tick value for the axis.  The strategy is to
     * display as many ticks as possible (selected from an array of 'standard'
     * tick units) without the labels overlapping.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area defined by the axes.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     */
Method code:
1069 |    protected void selectHorizontalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D g2,
1070 |                                                Rectangle2D dataArea,
1071 |                                                RectangleEdge edge) {
1072 | 
1073 |         double tickLabelWidth = estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(
1074 |             g2, getTickUnit()
1075 |         );
1076 | 
1077 |         // start with the current tick unit...
1078 |         TickUnitSource tickUnits = getStandardTickUnits();
1079 |         TickUnit unit1 = tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(getTickUnit());
1080 |         double unit1Width = lengthToJava2D(unit1.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1081 | 
1082 |         // then extrapolate...
1083 |         double guess = (tickLabelWidth / unit1Width) * unit1.getSize();
1084 | 
1085 |         NumberTickUnit unit2 
1086 |             = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(guess);
1087 |         double unit2Width = lengthToJava2D(unit2.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1088 | 
1089 |         tickLabelWidth = estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(g2, unit2);
1090 |         if (tickLabelWidth > unit2Width) {
1091 |             unit2 = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getLargerTickUnit(unit2);
1092 |         }
1093 | 
1094 |         setTickUnit(unit2, false, false);
1095 | 
1096 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.data.Range.expandToInclude(Range,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/Range.java
Original range: 227-240
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns a range that includes all the values in the specified 
     * <code>range</code> AND the specified <code>value</code>.
     * 
     * @param range  the range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param value  the value that must be included.
     * 
     * @return A range.
     * 
     * @since 1.0.1
     */
Method code:
227 |     public static Range expandToInclude(Range range, double value) {
228 |         if (range == null) {
229 |             return new Range(value, value);
230 |         }
231 |         if (value < range.getLowerBound()) {
232 |             return new Range(value, range.getUpperBound());
233 |         }
234 |         else if (value > range.getUpperBound()) {
235 |             return new Range(range.getLowerBound(), value);
236 |         }
237 |         else {
238 |             return range;
239 |         }
240 |     }

===== M0416 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.refreshTicksHorizontal(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 1172-1231
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the positions of the tick labels for the axis, storing the 
     * results in the tick label list (ready for drawing).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area in which the data should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the location of the axis.
     * 
     * @return A list of ticks.
     */
Method code:
1172 |     protected List refreshTicksHorizontal(Graphics2D g2,
1173 |                                           Rectangle2D dataArea,
1174 |                                           RectangleEdge edge) {
1175 | 
1176 |         List result = new java.util.ArrayList();
1177 | 
1178 |         Font tickLabelFont = getTickLabelFont();
1179 |         g2.setFont(tickLabelFont);
1180 |         
1181 |         if (isAutoTickUnitSelection()) {
1182 |             selectAutoTickUnit(g2, dataArea, edge);
1183 |         }
1184 | 
1185 |         double size = getTickUnit().getSize();
1186 |         int count = calculateVisibleTickCount();
1187 |         double lowestTickValue = calculateLowestVisibleTickValue();
1188 | 
1189 |         if (count <= ValueAxis.MAXIMUM_TICK_COUNT) {
1190 |             for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
1191 |                 double currentTickValue = lowestTickValue + (i * size);
1192 |                 String tickLabel;
1193 |                 NumberFormat formatter = getNumberFormatOverride();
1194 |                 if (formatter != null) {
1195 |                     tickLabel = formatter.format(currentTickValue);
1196 |                 }
1197 |                 else {
1198 |                     tickLabel = getTickUnit().valueToString(currentTickValue);
1199 |                 }
1200 |                 TextAnchor anchor = null;
1201 |                 TextAnchor rotationAnchor = null;
1202 |                 double angle = 0.0;
1203 |                 if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1204 |                     anchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1205 |                     rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1206 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
1207 |                         angle = Math.PI / 2.0;
1208 |                     }
1209 |                     else {
1210 |                         angle = -Math.PI / 2.0;
1211 |                     }
1212 |                 }
1213 |                 else {
1214 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
1215 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1216 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1217 |                     }
1218 |                     else {
1219 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER;
1220 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER;
1221 |                     }
1222 |                 }
1223 | 
1224 |                 Tick tick = new NumberTick(new Double(currentTickValue), 
1225 |                         tickLabel, anchor, rotationAnchor, angle);
1226 |                 result.add(tick);
1227 |             }
1228 |         }
1229 |         return result;
1230 | 
1231 |     }

/**
     * Arranges the blocks without any constraints.  This puts all blocks
     * into a single row.
     * 
     * @param container  the container.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The size after the arrangement.
     */
Method code:
358 |     protected Size2D arrangeNN(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2) {
359 |         double x = 0.0;
360 |         double width = 0.0;
361 |         double maxHeight = 0.0;
362 |         List blocks = container.getBlocks();
363 |         int blockCount = blocks.size();
364 |         if (blockCount > 0) {
365 |             Size2D[] sizes = new Size2D[blocks.size()];
366 |             for (int i = 0; i < blocks.size(); i++) {
367 |                 Block block = (Block) blocks.get(i);
368 |                 sizes[i] = block.arrange(g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE);
369 |                 width = width + sizes[i].getWidth();
370 |                 maxHeight = Math.max(sizes[i].height, maxHeight);
371 |                 block.setBounds(
372 |                     new Rectangle2D.Double(
373 |                         x, 0.0, sizes[i].width, sizes[i].height
374 |                     )
375 |                 );
376 |                 x = x + sizes[i].width + this.horizontalGap;
377 |             }
378 |             if (blockCount > 1) {
379 |                 width = width + this.horizontalGap * (blockCount - 1);   
380 |             }
381 |             if (this.verticalAlignment != VerticalAlignment.TOP) {
382 |                 for (int i = 0; i < blocks.size(); i++) {
383 |                     //Block b = (Block) blocks.get(i);
384 |                     if (this.verticalAlignment == VerticalAlignment.CENTER) {
385 |                         //TODO: shift block down by half
386 |                     }
387 |                     else if (this.verticalAlignment 
388 |                             == VerticalAlignment.BOTTOM) {
389 |                         //TODO: shift block down to bottom
390 |                     }
391 |                 }            
392 |             }
393 |         }
394 |         return new Size2D(width, maxHeight);
395 |     }

===== M0419 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.LineBorder.draw(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/LineBorder.java
Original range: 143-179
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws the border by filling in the reserved space (in black).
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param area  the area.
     */
Method code:
143 |     public void draw(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area) {
144 |         double w = area.getWidth();
145 |         double h = area.getHeight();
146 |         // if the area has zero height or width, we shouldn't draw anything
147 |         if (w <= 0.0 || h <= 0.0) {
148 |             return;
149 |         }
150 |         double t = this.insets.calculateTopInset(h);
151 |         double b = this.insets.calculateBottomInset(h);
152 |         double l = this.insets.calculateLeftInset(w);
153 |         double r = this.insets.calculateRightInset(w);
154 |         double x = area.getX();
155 |         double y = area.getY();
156 |         double x0 = x + l / 2.0;
157 |         double x1 = x + w - r / 2.0;
158 |         double y0 = y + h - b / 2.0;
159 |         double y1 = y + t / 2.0;
160 |         g2.setPaint(getPaint());
161 |         g2.setStroke(getStroke());

/**
     * Creates an array of standard shapes to display for the items in series 
     * on charts.
     *
     * @return The array of shapes.
     */
Method code:
289 |     public static Shape[] createStandardSeriesShapes() {
290 | 
291 |         Shape[] result = new Shape[10];
292 | 
293 |         double size = 6.0;
294 |         double delta = size / 2.0;
295 |         int[] xpoints = null;
296 |         int[] ypoints = null;
297 | 
298 |         // square
299 |         result[0] = new Rectangle2D.Double(-delta, -delta, size, size);
300 |         // circle
301 |         result[1] = new Ellipse2D.Double(-delta, -delta, size, size);
302 |

304 |         xpoints = intArray(0.0, delta, -delta);
305 |         ypoints = intArray(-delta, delta, delta);
306 |         result[2] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 3);
307 |

309 |         xpoints = intArray(0.0, delta, 0.0, -delta);
310 |         ypoints = intArray(-delta, 0.0, delta, 0.0);
311 |         result[3] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 4);
312 | 
313 |         // horizontal rectangle
314 |         result[4] = new Rectangle2D.Double(-delta, -delta / 2, size, size / 2);
315 | 
316 |         // down-pointing triangle
317 |         xpoints = intArray(-delta, +delta, 0.0);
318 |         ypoints = intArray(-delta, -delta, delta);
319 |         result[5] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 3);
320 | 
321 |         // horizontal ellipse
322 |         result[6] = new Ellipse2D.Double(-delta, -delta / 2, size, size / 2);
323 | 
324 |         // right-pointing triangle
325 |         xpoints = intArray(-delta, delta, -delta);
326 |         ypoints = intArray(-delta, 0.0, delta);
327 |         result[7] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 3);
328 | 
329 |         // vertical rectangle
330 |         result[8] = new Rectangle2D.Double(-delta / 2, -delta, size / 2, size);
331 | 
332 |         // left-pointing triangle
333 |         xpoints = intArray(-delta, delta, delta);
334 |         ypoints = intArray(0.0, -delta, +delta);
335 |         result[9] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 3);
336 | 
337 |         return result;
338 | 
339 |     }

/**
     * A utility method for determining the height of the tallest tick label.
     *
     * @param ticks  the ticks.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param drawArea  the area within which the plot and axes should be drawn.
     * @param vertical  a flag that indicates whether or not the tick labels 
     *                  are 'vertical'.
     *
     * @return The height of the tallest tick label.
     */
Method code:
815 |     protected double findMaximumTickLabelHeight(List ticks,
816 |                                                 Graphics2D g2, 
817 |                                                 Rectangle2D drawArea, 
818 |                                                 boolean vertical) {
819 |                                                     
820 |         RectangleInsets insets = getTickLabelInsets();
821 |         Font font = getTickLabelFont();
822 |         double maxHeight = 0.0;
823 |         if (vertical) {
824 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(font);
825 |             Iterator iterator = ticks.iterator();
826 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
827 |                 Tick tick = (Tick) iterator.next();
828 |                 Rectangle2D labelBounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(
829 |                         tick.getText(), g2, fm);
830 |                 if (labelBounds.getWidth() + insets.getTop() 
831 |                         + insets.getBottom() > maxHeight) {
832 |                     maxHeight = labelBounds.getWidth() 
833 |                                 + insets.getTop() + insets.getBottom();
834 |                 }
835 |             }
836 |         }
837 |         else {
838 |             LineMetrics metrics = font.getLineMetrics("ABCxyz", 
839 |                     g2.getFontRenderContext());
840 |             maxHeight = metrics.getHeight() 
841 |                         + insets.getTop() + insets.getBottom();
842 |         }
843 |         return maxHeight;
844 |         
845 |     }

===== M0443 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.selectVerticalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 1107-1132
Documentation:
/**
     * Selects an appropriate tick value for the axis.  The strategy is to
     * display as many ticks as possible (selected from an array of 'standard'
     * tick units) without the labels overlapping.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area in which the plot should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     */
Method code:
1107 |     protected void selectVerticalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D g2, 
1108 |                                               Rectangle2D dataArea, 
1109 |                                               RectangleEdge edge) {
1110 | 
1111 |         double tickLabelHeight = estimateMaximumTickLabelHeight(g2);
1112 | 
1113 |         // start with the current tick unit...
1114 |         TickUnitSource tickUnits = getStandardTickUnits();
1115 |         TickUnit unit1 = tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(getTickUnit());
1116 |         double unitHeight = lengthToJava2D(unit1.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1117 | 
1118 |         // then extrapolate...
1119 |         double guess = (tickLabelHeight / unitHeight) * unit1.getSize();
1120 |         
1121 |         NumberTickUnit unit2 
1122 |             = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(guess);
1123 |         double unit2Height = lengthToJava2D(unit2.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1124 | 
1125 |         tickLabelHeight = estimateMaximumTickLabelHeight(g2);
1126 |         if (tickLabelHeight > unit2Height) {
1127 |             unit2 = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getLargerTickUnit(unit2);
1128 |         }
1129 | 
1130 |         setTickUnit(unit2, false, false);
1131 | 
1132 |     }

/**
     * Draws the text block, aligning it with the specified anchor point and 
     * rotating it about the specified rotation point.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.

===== M0457 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.createTextBlock(String,Font,Paint)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 114-149
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a {@link TextBlock} from a <code>String</code>.  Line breaks 
     * are added where the <code>String</code> contains '\n' characters.
     * 
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param font  the font.
     * @param paint  the paint.
     * 
     * @return A text block.
     */
Method code:
114 |     public static TextBlock createTextBlock(String text, Font font,
115 |                                             Paint paint) {
116 |         if (text == null) {
117 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'text' argument.");
118 |         }
119 |         TextBlock result = new TextBlock();
120 |         String input = text;
121 |         boolean moreInputToProcess = (text.length() > 0);
122 |         int start = 0;
123 |         while (moreInputToProcess) {
124 |             int index = input.indexOf("\n");
125 |             if (index > start) {
126 |                 String line = input.substring(start, index);
127 |                 if (index < input.length() - 1) {
128 |                     result.addLine(line, font, paint);
129 |                     input = input.substring(index + 1);
130 |                 }
131 |                 else {
132 |                     moreInputToProcess = false;
133 |                 }
134 |             }
135 |             else if (index == start) {
136 |                 if (index < input.length() - 1) {
137 |                     input = input.substring(index + 1);
138 |                 }
139 |                 else {
140 |                     moreInputToProcess = false;
141 |                 }
142 |             }
143 |             else {
144 |                 result.addLine(input, font, paint);
145 |                 moreInputToProcess = false;
146 |             }
147 |         }
148 |         return result;
149 |     }

===== M0468 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.deriveRotationAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D,String,TextAnchor)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 688-770
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method that calculates the rotation anchor offsets for a 
     * string.  These offsets are relative to the text starting coordinate 
     * (BASELINE_LEFT).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param anchor  the anchor point.
     *
     * @return  The offsets.
     */
Method code:
688 |     private static float[] deriveRotationAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D g2,
689 |             String text, TextAnchor anchor) {
690 | 
691 |         float[] result = new float[2];
692 |         FontRenderContext frc = g2.getFontRenderContext();
693 |         LineMetrics metrics = g2.getFont().getLineMetrics(text, frc);
694 |         FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics();
695 |         Rectangle2D bounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(text, g2, fm);
696 |         float ascent = metrics.getAscent();
697 |         float halfAscent = ascent / 2.0f;
698 |         float descent = metrics.getDescent();
699 |         float leading = metrics.getLeading();
700 |         float xAdj = 0.0f;
701 |         float yAdj = 0.0f;
702 | 
703 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT
704 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
705 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
706 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_LEFT
707 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_LEFT) {
708 | 
709 |             xAdj = 0.0f;
710 | 
711 |         }
712 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
713 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
714 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
715 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
716 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER) {
717 | 
718 |             xAdj = (float) bounds.getWidth() / 2.0f;
719 | 
720 |         }
721 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT
722 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT
723 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT
724 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT
725 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
726 | 
727 |             xAdj = (float) bounds.getWidth();
728 | 
729 |         }
730 | 
731 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT
732 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
733 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT) {
734 | 
735 |             yAdj = descent + leading - (float) bounds.getHeight();
736 | 
737 |         }
738 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
739 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
740 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT) {
741 | 
742 |             yAdj = descent + leading - (float) (bounds.getHeight() / 2.0);
743 | 
744 |         }
745 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_LEFT
746 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER
747 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
748 | 
749 |             yAdj = -halfAscent;
750 | 
751 |         }
752 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_LEFT
753 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
754 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT) {
755 | 
756 |             yAdj = 0.0f;
757 | 
758 |         }
759 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
760 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
761 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
762 | 
763 |             yAdj = metrics.getDescent() + metrics.getLeading();
764 | 
765 |         }
766 |         result[0] = xAdj;
767 |         result[1] = yAdj;
768 |         return result;
769 | 
770 |     }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.deriveTextBoundsAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D,String,TextAnchor)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 601-675
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method that calculates the anchor offsets for a string.  
     * Normally, the (x, y) coordinate for drawing text is a point on the 
     * baseline at the left of the text string.  If you add these offsets to 
     * (x, y) and draw the string, then the anchor point should coincide with 
     * the (x, y) point.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (not <code>null</code>).
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param anchor  the anchor point.
     *
     * @return  The offsets.
     */
Method code:
601 |     private static float[] deriveTextBoundsAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D g2,
602 |             String text, TextAnchor anchor) {
603 | 
604 |         float[] result = new float[2];
605 |         FontRenderContext frc = g2.getFontRenderContext();
606 |         Font f = g2.getFont();
607 |         FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(f);
608 |         Rectangle2D bounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(text, g2, fm);
609 |         LineMetrics metrics = f.getLineMetrics(text, frc);
610 |         float ascent = metrics.getAscent();
611 |         float halfAscent = ascent / 2.0f;
612 |         float descent = metrics.getDescent();
613 |         float leading = metrics.getLeading();
614 |         float xAdj = 0.0f;
615 |         float yAdj = 0.0f;
616 | 
617 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
618 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
619 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
620 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
621 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER) {
622 | 
623 |             xAdj = (float) -bounds.getWidth() / 2.0f;
624 | 
625 |         }
626 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT
627 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT
628 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT
629 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT
630 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
631 | 
632 |             xAdj = (float) -bounds.getWidth();
633 | 
634 |         }
635 | 
636 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT
637 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
638 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT) {
639 | 
640 |             yAdj = -descent - leading + (float) bounds.getHeight();
641 | 
642 |         }
643 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_LEFT
644 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER
645 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
646 | 
647 |             yAdj = halfAscent;
648 | 
649 |         }
650 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
651 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
652 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT) {
653 | 
654 |             yAdj = -descent - leading + (float) (bounds.getHeight() / 2.0);
655 | 
656 |         }
657 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_LEFT
658 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
659 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT) {
660 | 
661 |             yAdj = 0.0f;
662 | 
663 |         }
664 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
665 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
666 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
667 | 
668 |             yAdj = -metrics.getDescent() - metrics.getLeading();
669 | 
670 |         }
671 |         result[0] = xAdj;
672 |         result[1] = yAdj;
673 |         return result;
674 | 
675 |     }

/**
     * Draws the chart on a Java 2D graphics device (such as the screen or a
     * printer).
     * <P>
     * This method is the focus of the entire JFreeChart library.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param chartArea  the area within which the chart should be drawn.

Method code:

1133 |                      Rectangle2D chartArea, Point2D anchor, 
1134 |                      ChartRenderingInfo info) {
1135 |

1177 |                         chartArea.getWidth() - 1.0, chartArea.getHeight()

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.ValueAxis.drawTickMarksAndLabels(Graphics2D,double,Rectangle2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge,PlotRenderingInfo)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/ValueAxis.java
Original range: 660-737
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws the axis line, tick marks and tick mark labels.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param cursor  the cursor.
     * @param plotArea  the plot area.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param edge  the edge that the axis is aligned with.
     * @param info  the plot rendering info.
     * 
     * @return The width or height used to draw the axis.
     */
Method code:
660 |     protected AxisState drawTickMarksAndLabels(Graphics2D g2, double cursor,
661 |             Rectangle2D plotArea, Rectangle2D dataArea, RectangleEdge edge,
662 |             PlotRenderingInfo info) {
663 |                                               
664 |         AxisState state = new AxisState(cursor);
665 | 
666 |         if (isAxisLineVisible()) {
667 |             drawAxisLine(g2, cursor, dataArea, edge);
668 |         }
669 | 
670 |         double ol = getTickMarkOutsideLength();
671 |         double il = getTickMarkInsideLength();
672 | 
673 |         List ticks = refreshTicks(g2, state, dataArea, edge);
674 |         state.setTicks(ticks);
675 |         g2.setFont(getTickLabelFont());
676 |         Iterator iterator = ticks.iterator();
677 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
678 |             ValueTick tick = (ValueTick) iterator.next();
679 |             if (isTickLabelsVisible()) {
680 |                 g2.setPaint(getTickLabelPaint());
681 |                 float[] anchorPoint = calculateAnchorPoint(tick, cursor, 
682 |                         dataArea, edge);
683 |                 TextUtilities.drawRotatedString(tick.getText(), g2, 
684 |                         anchorPoint[0], anchorPoint[1], tick.getTextAnchor(), 
685 |                         tick.getAngle(), tick.getRotationAnchor());
686 |             }
687 | 
688 |             if (isTickMarksVisible() && tick.getTickType().equals(TickType.MAJOR)) {
689 |                 float xx = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, 
690 |                         edge);
691 |                 Line2D mark = null;
692 |                 g2.setStroke(getTickMarkStroke());
693 |                 g2.setPaint(getTickMarkPaint());
694 |                 if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
695 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(cursor - ol, xx, cursor + il, xx);
696 |                 }
697 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
698 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(cursor + ol, xx, cursor - il, xx);
699 |                 }
700 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
701 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(xx, cursor - ol, xx, cursor + il);
702 |                 }
703 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
704 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(xx, cursor + ol, xx, cursor - il);
705 |                 }
706 |                 g2.draw(mark);
707 |             }
708 |         }
709 |         
710 |         // need to work out the space used by the tick labels...
711 |         // so we can update the cursor...
712 |         double used = 0.0;
713 |         if (isTickLabelsVisible()) {
714 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
715 |                 used += findMaximumTickLabelWidth(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
716 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());  
717 |                 state.cursorLeft(used);      
718 |             }
719 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
720 |                 used = findMaximumTickLabelWidth(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
721 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
722 |                 state.cursorRight(used);      
723 |             }
724 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
725 |                 used = findMaximumTickLabelHeight(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
726 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
727 |                 state.cursorUp(used);
728 |             }
729 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
730 |                 used = findMaximumTickLabelHeight(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
731 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
732 |                 state.cursorDown(used);
733 |             }
734 |         }
735 |        
736 |         return state;
737 |     }

Method code:
474 |     public static void drawRotatedString(String text, Graphics2D g2,
475 |             float textX, float textY, double angle,
476 |             float rotateX, float rotateY) {
477 | 
478 |         if ((text == null) || (text.equals(""))) {
479 |             return;
480 |         }
481 | 
482 |         AffineTransform saved = g2.getTransform();
483 | 
484 |         // apply the rotation...
485 |         AffineTransform rotate = AffineTransform.getRotateInstance(
486 |                 angle, rotateX, rotateY);
487 |         g2.transform(rotate);
488 | 
489 |         if (useDrawRotatedStringWorkaround) {
490 |             // workaround for JDC bug ID 4312117 and others...
491 |             TextLayout tl = new TextLayout(text, g2.getFont(), 
492 |                     g2.getFontRenderContext());
493 |             tl.draw(g2, textX, textY);
494 |         }
495 |         else {
496 |             // replaces this code...
497 |             g2.drawString(text, textX, textY);
498 |         }
499 |         g2.setTransform(saved);
500 | 
501 |     }

/**
     * Calculates the anchor point for a tick label.
     * 
     * @param tick  the tick.
     * @param cursor  the cursor.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param edge  the edge on which the axis is drawn.
     * 
     * @return The x and y coordinates of the anchor point.
     */
Method code:
622 |     protected float[] calculateAnchorPoint(ValueTick tick, 
623 |                                            double cursor, 
624 |                                            Rectangle2D dataArea, 
625 |                                            RectangleEdge edge) {
626 |     
627 |         RectangleInsets insets = getTickLabelInsets();
628 |         float[] result = new float[2];
629 |         if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
630 |             result[0] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
631 |             result[1] = (float) (cursor - insets.getBottom() - 2.0);
632 |         }
633 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
634 |             result[0] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
635 |             result[1] = (float) (cursor + insets.getTop() + 2.0); 
636 |         }
637 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
638 |             result[0] = (float) (cursor - insets.getLeft() - 2.0);    
639 |             result[1] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
640 |         }
641 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
642 |             result[0] = (float) (cursor + insets.getRight() + 2.0);    
643 |             result[1] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
644 |         }
645 |         return result;
646 |     }

===== M0502 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.ValueAxis.findMaximumTickLabelWidth(List,Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/ValueAxis.java
Original range: 858-888
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method for determining the width of the widest tick label.
     *
     * @param ticks  the ticks.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param drawArea  the area within which the plot and axes should be drawn.
     * @param vertical  a flag that indicates whether or not the tick labels 
     *                  are 'vertical'.
     *
     * @return The width of the tallest tick label.
     */
Method code:
858 |     protected double findMaximumTickLabelWidth(List ticks, 
859 |                                                Graphics2D g2, 
860 |                                                Rectangle2D drawArea, 
861 |                                                boolean vertical) {
862 |                                                    
863 |         RectangleInsets insets = getTickLabelInsets();
864 |         Font font = getTickLabelFont();
865 |         double maxWidth = 0.0;
866 |         if (!vertical) {
867 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(font);
868 |             Iterator iterator = ticks.iterator();
869 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
870 |                 Tick tick = (Tick) iterator.next();
871 |                 Rectangle2D labelBounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(
872 |                         tick.getText(), g2, fm);
873 |                 if (labelBounds.getWidth() + insets.getLeft() 
874 |                         + insets.getRight() > maxWidth) {
875 |                     maxWidth = labelBounds.getWidth() 
876 |                                + insets.getLeft() + insets.getRight();
877 |                 }
878 |             }
879 |         }
880 |         else {
881 |             LineMetrics metrics = font.getLineMetrics("ABCxyz", 
882 |                     g2.getFontRenderContext());
883 |             maxWidth = metrics.getHeight() 
884 |                        + insets.getTop() + insets.getBottom();
885 |         }
886 |         return maxWidth;
887 |         
888 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.refreshTicksVertical(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 1244-1308
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the positions of the tick labels for the axis, storing the 
     * results in the tick label list (ready for drawing).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area in which the plot should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the location of the axis.
     * 
     * @return A list of ticks.
     *
     */
Method code:
1244 |     protected List refreshTicksVertical(Graphics2D g2,
1245 |                                         Rectangle2D dataArea,
1246 |                                         RectangleEdge edge) {
1247 | 
1248 |         List result = new java.util.ArrayList();
1249 |         result.clear();
1250 | 
1251 |         Font tickLabelFont = getTickLabelFont();
1252 |         g2.setFont(tickLabelFont);
1253 |         if (isAutoTickUnitSelection()) {
1254 |             selectAutoTickUnit(g2, dataArea, edge);
1255 |         }
1256 | 
1257 |         double size = getTickUnit().getSize();
1258 |         int count = calculateVisibleTickCount();
1259 |         double lowestTickValue = calculateLowestVisibleTickValue();
1260 | 
1261 |         if (count <= ValueAxis.MAXIMUM_TICK_COUNT) {
1262 |             for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
1263 |                 double currentTickValue = lowestTickValue + (i * size);
1264 |                 String tickLabel;
1265 |                 NumberFormat formatter = getNumberFormatOverride();
1266 |                 if (formatter != null) {
1267 |                     tickLabel = formatter.format(currentTickValue);
1268 |                 }
1269 |                 else {
1270 |                     tickLabel = getTickUnit().valueToString(currentTickValue);
1271 |                 }
1272 | 
1273 |                 TextAnchor anchor = null;
1274 |                 TextAnchor rotationAnchor = null;
1275 |                 double angle = 0.0;
1276 |                 if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1277 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) { 
1278 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1279 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1280 |                         angle = -Math.PI / 2.0;
1281 |                     }
1282 |                     else {
1283 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1284 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1285 |                         angle = Math.PI / 2.0;
1286 |                     }
1287 |                 }
1288 |                 else {
1289 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
1290 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1291 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1292 |                     }
1293 |                     else {
1294 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT;
1295 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT;
1296 |                     }
1297 |                 }
1298 | 
1299 |                 Tick tick = new NumberTick(
1300 |                     new Double(currentTickValue), tickLabel, anchor, 
1301 |                     rotationAnchor, angle
1302 |                 );
1303 |                 result.add(tick);
1304 |             }
1305 |         }
1306 |         return result;
1307 | 
1308 |     }

521 |     public void trim(Rectangle2D area) {

===== M0533 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.isEmptyOrNull(CategoryDataset)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 562-585
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns <code>true</code> if the dataset is empty (or <code>null</code>),
     * and <code>false</code> otherwise.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> permitted).
     *
     * @return A boolean.
     */
Method code:
562 |     public static boolean isEmptyOrNull(CategoryDataset dataset) {
563 | 
564 |         if (dataset == null) {
565 |             return true;
566 |         }
567 | 
568 |         int rowCount = dataset.getRowCount();
569 |         int columnCount = dataset.getColumnCount();
570 |         if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
571 |             return true;
572 |         }
573 | 
574 |         for (int r = 0; r < rowCount; r++) {
575 |             for (int c = 0; c < columnCount; c++) {
576 |                 if (dataset.getValue(r, c) != null) {
577 |                     return false;
578 |                 }
579 | 
580 |             }
581 |         }
582 | 
583 |         return true;
584 | 
585 |     }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(Graphics2D,TickUnit)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 999-1036
Documentation:
/**
     * Estimates the maximum width of the tick labels, assuming the specified 
     * tick unit is used.
     * <P>
     * Rather than computing the string bounds of every tick on the axis, we 
     * just look at two values: the lower bound and the upper bound for the 
     * axis.  These two values will usually be representative.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param unit  the tick unit to use for calculation.
     *
     * @return The estimated maximum width of the tick labels.
     */
Method code:
999 |     protected double estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(Graphics2D g2, 
1000 |                                                    TickUnit unit) {
1001 | 
1002 |         RectangleInsets tickLabelInsets = getTickLabelInsets();
1003 |         double result = tickLabelInsets.getLeft() + tickLabelInsets.getRight();
1004 | 
1005 |         if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1006 |             // all tick labels have the same width (equal to the height of the 
1007 |             // font)...
1008 |             FontRenderContext frc = g2.getFontRenderContext();
1009 |             LineMetrics lm = getTickLabelFont().getLineMetrics("0", frc);
1010 |             result += lm.getHeight();
1011 |         }
1012 |         else {
1013 |             // look at lower and upper bounds...
1014 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(getTickLabelFont());
1015 |             Range range = getRange();
1016 |             double lower = range.getLowerBound();
1017 |             double upper = range.getUpperBound();
1018 |             String lowerStr = "";
1019 |             String upperStr = "";
1020 |             NumberFormat formatter = getNumberFormatOverride();
1021 |             if (formatter != null) {
1022 |                 lowerStr = formatter.format(lower);
1023 |                 upperStr = formatter.format(upper);
1024 |             }
1025 |             else {
1026 |                 lowerStr = unit.valueToString(lower);
1027 |                 upperStr = unit.valueToString(upper);                
1028 |             }
1029 |             double w1 = fm.stringWidth(lowerStr);
1030 |             double w2 = fm.stringWidth(upperStr);
1031 |             result += Math.max(w1, w2);
1032 |         }
1033 | 
1034 |         return result;
1035 | 
1036 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.calculateCategoryGapSize(int,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 697-718
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the size (width or height, depending on the location of the 
     * axis) of a category gap.
     *
     * @param categoryCount  the number of categories.
     * @param area  the area within which the categories will be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The category gap width.
     */
Method code:
697 |     protected double calculateCategoryGapSize(int categoryCount, 
698 |                                               Rectangle2D area,
699 |                                               RectangleEdge edge) {
700 | 
701 |         double result = 0.0;
702 |         double available = 0.0;
703 | 
704 |         if ((edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) || (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM)) {
705 |             available = area.getWidth();
706 |         }
707 |         else if ((edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) 
708 |                 || (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT)) {
709 |             available = area.getHeight();
710 |         }
711 | 
712 |         if (categoryCount > 1) {
713 |             result = available * getCategoryMargin() / (categoryCount - 1);
714 |         }
715 | 
716 |         return result;
717 | 
718 |     }

/**
     * Draws the axis label.

1092 |     protected AxisState drawLabel(String label, Graphics2D g2,

1094 |             AxisState state, PlotRenderingInfo plotState) {
1095 |

1097 |         if (state == null) {
1098 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'state' argument.");
1099 |         }
1100 |

1102 |             return state;

1112 |

1115 |                     getLabelAngle(), labelBounds.getCenterX(),

1117 |             Shape rotatedLabelBounds = t.createTransformedShape(labelBounds);
1118 |             labelBounds = rotatedLabelBounds.getBounds2D();
1119 |             float w = (float) labelBounds.getWidth();
1120 |             float h = (float) labelBounds.getHeight();
1121 |             float labelx = (float) dataArea.getCenterX();
1122 |             float labely = (float) (state.getCursor() - insets.getBottom() 
1123 |                     - h / 2.0);
1124 |             TextUtilities.drawRotatedString(label, g2, labelx, labely, 
1125 |                     TextAnchor.CENTER, getLabelAngle(), TextAnchor.CENTER);
1126 |             hotspot = new Rectangle2D.Float(labelx - w / 2.0f, 
1127 |                     labely - h / 2.0f, w, h);
1128 |             state.cursorUp(insets.getTop() + labelBounds.getHeight() 
1129 |                     + insets.getBottom());
1130 |         }
1131 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
1132 |             AffineTransform t = AffineTransform.getRotateInstance(
1133 |                     getLabelAngle(), labelBounds.getCenterX(), 
1134 |                     labelBounds.getCenterY());
1135 |             Shape rotatedLabelBounds = t.createTransformedShape(labelBounds);
1136 |             labelBounds = rotatedLabelBounds.getBounds2D();
1137 |             float w = (float) labelBounds.getWidth();
1138 |             float h = (float) labelBounds.getHeight();
1139 |             float labelx = (float) dataArea.getCenterX();
1140 |             float labely = (float) (state.getCursor() + insets.getTop() 
1141 |                     + h / 2.0);

1143 |                     TextAnchor.CENTER, getLabelAngle(), TextAnchor.CENTER);

1166 |                     + insets.getRight());
1167 |         }

1171 |                     getLabelAngle() + Math.PI / 2.0, 
1172 |                     labelBounds.getCenterX(), labelBounds.getCenterY());
1173 |             Shape rotatedLabelBounds = t.createTransformedShape(labelBounds);

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.getCategoryStart(int,int,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 588-608
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the starting coordinate for the specified category.
     *
     * @param category  the category.
     * @param categoryCount  the number of categories.
     * @param area  the data area.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The coordinate.
     * 
     * @see #getCategoryMiddle(int, int, Rectangle2D, RectangleEdge)
     * @see #getCategoryEnd(int, int, Rectangle2D, RectangleEdge)
     */
Method code:
588 |     public double getCategoryStart(int category, int categoryCount, 
589 |                                    Rectangle2D area,
590 |                                    RectangleEdge edge) {
591 | 
592 |         double result = 0.0;
593 |         if ((edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) || (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM)) {
594 |             result = area.getX() + area.getWidth() * getLowerMargin();
595 |         }
596 |         else if ((edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) 
597 |                 || (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT)) {
598 |             result = area.getMinY() + area.getHeight() * getLowerMargin();
599 |         }
600 | 
601 |         double categorySize = calculateCategorySize(categoryCount, area, edge);
602 |         double categoryGapWidth = calculateCategoryGapSize(categoryCount, area,
603 |                 edge);
604 | 
605 |         result = result + category * (categorySize + categoryGapWidth);
606 |         return result;
607 |         
608 |     }

===== M0585 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.autoAdjustRange()
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 421-507
Documentation:
/**
     * Rescales the axis to ensure that all data is visible.
     */
Method code:
421 |     protected void autoAdjustRange() {
422 | 
423 |         Plot plot = getPlot();
424 |         if (plot == null) {
425 |             return;  // no plot, no data
426 |         }
427 | 
428 |         if (plot instanceof ValueAxisPlot) {
429 |             ValueAxisPlot vap = (ValueAxisPlot) plot;
430 | 
431 |             Range r = vap.getDataRange(this);
432 |             if (r == null) {
433 |                 r = getDefaultAutoRange();
434 |             }
435 |             
436 |             double upper = r.getUpperBound();
437 |             double lower = r.getLowerBound();
438 |             if (this.rangeType == RangeType.POSITIVE) {
439 |                 lower = Math.max(0.0, lower);
440 |                 upper = Math.max(0.0, upper);
441 |             }
442 |             else if (this.rangeType == RangeType.NEGATIVE) {
443 |                 lower = Math.min(0.0, lower);
444 |                 upper = Math.min(0.0, upper);                   
445 |             }
446 |             
447 |             if (getAutoRangeIncludesZero()) {
448 |                 lower = Math.min(lower, 0.0);
449 |                 upper = Math.max(upper, 0.0);
450 |             }
451 |             double range = upper - lower;
452 | 
453 |             // if fixed auto range, then derive lower bound...
454 |             double fixedAutoRange = getFixedAutoRange();
455 |             if (fixedAutoRange > 0.0) {
456 |                 lower = upper - fixedAutoRange;
457 |             }
458 |             else {
459 |                 // ensure the autorange is at least <minRange> in size...
460 |                 double minRange = getAutoRangeMinimumSize();
461 |                 if (range < minRange) {
462 |                     double expand = (minRange - range) / 2;
463 |                     upper = upper + expand;
464 |                     lower = lower - expand;
465 |                     if (lower == upper) { // see bug report 1549218
466 |                         double adjust = Math.abs(lower) / 10.0;
467 |                         lower = lower - adjust;
468 |                         upper = upper + adjust;
469 |                     }
470 |                     if (this.rangeType == RangeType.POSITIVE) {
471 |                         if (lower < 0.0) {
472 |                             upper = upper - lower;
473 |                             lower = 0.0;
474 |                         }
475 |                     }
476 |                     else if (this.rangeType == RangeType.NEGATIVE) {
477 |                         if (upper > 0.0) {
478 |                             lower = lower - upper;
479 |                             upper = 0.0;
480 |                         }
481 |                     }
482 |                 }
483 | 
484 |                 if (getAutoRangeStickyZero()) {
485 |                     if (upper <= 0.0) {
486 |                         upper = Math.min(0.0, upper + getUpperMargin() * range);
487 |                     }
488 |                     else {
489 |                         upper = upper + getUpperMargin() * range;
490 |                     }
491 |                     if (lower >= 0.0) {
492 |                         lower = Math.max(0.0, lower - getLowerMargin() * range);
493 |                     }
494 |                     else {
495 |                         lower = lower - getLowerMargin() * range;
496 |                     }
497 |                 }
498 |                 else {
499 |                     upper = upper + getUpperMargin() * range;
500 |                     lower = lower - getLowerMargin() * range;
501 |                 }
502 |             }
503 | 
504 |             setRange(new Range(lower, upper), false, false);
505 |         }
506 | 
507 |     }

===== M0587 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.calculateCategorySize(int,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 662-685
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the size (width or height, depending on the location of the 
     * axis) of a category.
     *
     * @param categoryCount  the number of categories.
     * @param area  the area within which the categories will be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The category size.
     */
Method code:
662 |     protected double calculateCategorySize(int categoryCount, Rectangle2D area,
663 |                                            RectangleEdge edge) {
664 | 
665 |         double result = 0.0;
666 |         double available = 0.0;
667 | 
668 |         if ((edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) || (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM)) {
669 |             available = area.getWidth();
670 |         }
671 |         else if ((edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) 
672 |                 || (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT)) {
673 |             available = area.getHeight();
674 |         }
675 |         if (categoryCount > 1) {
676 |             result = available * (1 - getLowerMargin() - getUpperMargin() 
677 |                      - getCategoryMargin());
678 |             result = result / categoryCount;
679 |         }
680 |         else {
681 |             result = available * (1 - getLowerMargin() - getUpperMargin());
682 |         }
683 |         return result;
684 | 
685 |     }

/**
     * Calculates and sets the bounds of all the items in the specified 
     * container, subject to the given constraint.  The <code>Graphics2D</code>
     * can be used by some items (particularly items containing text) to 
     * calculate sizing parameters.
     * 
     * @param container  the container whose items are being arranged.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param constraint  the size constraint.
     * 
     * @return The size of the container after arrangement of the contents.
     */
Method code:
94 |     public Size2D arrange(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2,
95 |                           RectangleConstraint constraint) {
96 |         
97 |         LengthConstraintType w = constraint.getWidthConstraintType();
98 |         LengthConstraintType h = constraint.getHeightConstraintType();
99 |         if (w == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
100 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
101 |                 return arrangeNN(container, g2);  
102 |             }
103 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
104 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
105 |             }
106 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
107 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
108 |             }
109 |         }
110 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
111 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
112 |                 return arrangeFN(container, g2, constraint);  
113 |             }
114 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
115 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
116 |             }
117 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
118 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
119 |             }
120 |         }
121 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
122 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
123 |                 return arrangeRN(container, g2, constraint);  
124 |             }
125 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
126 |                 return arrangeRF(container, g2, constraint);  
127 |             }
128 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
129 |                 return arrangeRR(container, g2, constraint);   
130 |             }
131 |         }

133 |

===== M0593 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.BorderArrangement.arrangeNN(BlockContainer,Graphics2D)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/BorderArrangement.java
Original range: 186-260
Documentation:
/**
     * Performs an arrangement without constraints.
     * 
     * @param container  the container.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The container size after the arrangement.
     */
Method code:
186 |     protected Size2D arrangeNN(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2) {
187 |         double[] w = new double[5];
188 |         double[] h = new double[5];
189 |         if (this.topBlock != null) {
190 |             Size2D size = this.topBlock.arrange(
191 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
192 |             );
193 |             w[0] = size.width;
194 |             h[0] = size.height;
195 |         }
196 |         if (this.bottomBlock != null) {
197 |             Size2D size = this.bottomBlock.arrange(
198 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
199 |             );
200 |             w[1] = size.width;
201 |             h[1] = size.height;
202 |         }
203 |         if (this.leftBlock != null) {
204 |             Size2D size = this.leftBlock.arrange(
205 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
206 |             );
207 |             w[2] = size.width;
208 |             h[2] = size.height;
209 |        }
210 |         if (this.rightBlock != null) {
211 |             Size2D size = this.rightBlock.arrange(
212 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
213 |             );
214 |             w[3] = size.width;
215 |             h[3] = size.height;
216 |         }
217 |         
218 |         h[2] = Math.max(h[2], h[3]);
219 |         h[3] = h[2];
220 |         
221 |         if (this.centerBlock != null) {
222 |             Size2D size = this.centerBlock.arrange(
223 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
224 |             );
225 |             w[4] = size.width;
226 |             h[4] = size.height;
227 |         }
228 |         double width = Math.max(w[0], Math.max(w[1], w[2] + w[4] + w[3]));
229 |         double centerHeight = Math.max(h[2], Math.max(h[3], h[4]));
230 |         double height = h[0] + h[1] + centerHeight;
231 |         if (this.topBlock != null) {
232 |             this.topBlock.setBounds(
233 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(0.0, 0.0, width, h[0])
234 |             );
235 |         }
236 |         if (this.bottomBlock != null) {
237 |             this.bottomBlock.setBounds(
238 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(0.0, height - h[1], width, h[1])
239 |             );
240 |         }
241 |         if (this.leftBlock != null) {
242 |             this.leftBlock.setBounds(
243 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(0.0, h[0], w[2], centerHeight)
244 |             );
245 |         }
246 |         if (this.rightBlock != null) {
247 |             this.rightBlock.setBounds(
248 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(width - w[3], h[0], w[3], centerHeight)
249 |             );
250 |         }
